valava, vāraṇam puṟaviṟ keṇṭi nāḷiri māṭṭip peṭi nōkkiya peruntaku niliyi utukkāṇ; itaṉik kaṇṭum tāḻttuc cēṟal naṉṟaṉṟu; ātalāl, iḷiyar acii vēṇṭamar naṭiyar, meṉmela varuka; nām celaṟkut tīṇṭā vimuḷ tīṇṭi ēmati eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. pari, īṇṭuk kutirimēṟ cellum celaviṉmēṟṟu. virinta celavu iḷiyāriyum iḷippeytac ceyyu mātaliṉ virippari eṉac ciṟappittāṉ. palarāyk kuḻīi varum iḷiyāri vīṅkucelal iḷiya reṉṟār. kutirimēṟ celluvatāl uṭi nekiḻāmip poruṭṭu iṟukkiya kacciyi aviḻttu iḷippāṟal vēṇṭutaliṉ yāppu aviḻttu acivu tīrvu kūṟappaṭṭatu. kacci ceṟittut tāvaṭi yiṭṭu naṭattal maṟavoḻukka mātaliṉ, naṭik kaṭṭuppāṭṭit taḷarttiyavaḻi avaravarum tam viruppampōl kuntiyum kuṟunaṭi yiṭṭum cellu
tali vēṇṭamar naṭi eṉṟār. vimmuḷ, ākupeyarāl kūriya muḷ cerukappaṭṭa tāṟṟukkōl mēl niṉṟatu; erutukaḷi iyakkum kōlilum itu mikavum kuṟuki
yirukkum. kuruti tōṉṟak kuttutal tītu eṉṟaṟkut tīṇṭutal kūṟiṉār. ēmati, mati muṉṉiliyaci. urukkiya neyyip pālil teḷittavaḻi, aḵtu oḷiyum karuniṟamum koṇṭu kōṭukōṭāy ōṭum; kōḻiyiṉ kaḻuttum atu pōl irukkum eṉa aṟika. kāmaru, kāmam maru eṉap pirittu virumpattakka eṉap poruḷ koḷka. taki, aḻaku, keṇṭi, kiḷaṟi. vāyalakiṉ iṭiyē kuṟukkākap paṟṟiṉami tōṉṟak kavara māṭṭi eṉṟār. māṇpeṭi, kātal māṇpuṭiya peṭik kōḻi. peruntaku nili, perumita nili; āṇmi tōṉṟa nimirnta naṭiyumām.
viṉimēṟ ceṉṟa talivaṉ atu muṟṟiyatum taṉ iḷiyarit tērpaṇṇuka, mīḷkam eṉṟāṉāka, aṉivarum uvaki uḷḷattiṟ kiḷarnteḻak kutiri ivarntu virintu mīḷkiṉṟaṉar; iṭiyē talivaṉ pōtarum tērit toṭara māṭṭātu piṟpaṭṭami kaṇṭu, iḷiyar iṉic ciṟitu iḷippāṟi varuka eṉap paṇittami tōṉṟa, virippari varuntiya vīṅkucelal iḷiyar meṉmela varuka eṉṟum, pariyūrtalāl piṟakkum varuttamum kacciyiṉ iṟukkamum iḷiyari virinta celavukku iṭiyūṟu cevvaṉa eṉak karutiṉamiyiṉ, virippari varuntiya eṉṟum, kacci yāppaḻittu acii eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. iḷiyarākaliṉ kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟit tām virumpiyavāṟu viḷiyāṭi varuvar eṉpatu uṇarntu vēṇṭamar naṭiyar meṉmela varuka, yām muṟpaṭṭuc celkam eṉṟum, vimmuḷḷāṟ kuttip pariyic celuttuvatit talivaṉ virumpu
pavaṉallaṉ eṉṟaṟkut tīṇṭāvimmuḷ eṉṟum, nām virintu cēṟal vēṇṭu mātalāl ippoḻutu am muḷḷāṟ kutirikaḷic ciṟitē tūṇṭuka eṉpāṉ, nām celaṟku vimmuḷ tīṇṭi ēmati eṉṟum, tēr celuttutalil pākaṉuk kuḷḷa vaṉmiyit tāṉ aṟintu pārāṭṭupavaṉ pōlap pāka eṉṉātu valava eṉṟum icittāṉ. kāṉak kōḻi maṇal malirak keṇṭi nāḷiriyi māṭṭic celvatu, tāṉ pōntu viṉi muṟṟi mikka poruḷoṭu maṉinōkki mīḷutaliyum peṭiyiṉ kātalmāṇpi niṉintu cellum cēvaliṉ cemmānta naṭi, viṉimuṭivil taṉ maṉak kiḻiyil tōṉṟiya taṉ kātaliyiṉ kātal nalam taṉ uḷḷatti yūkkat tāṉ ataṉ vaḻiccellum ciṟappiyum ottiruppatu uṇarntu uvaki mikutiyāl, uriyāṭuvār piṟar  iṉmiyiṉ, pākaṉukkuk kāṉaccēvaliṉ peruntaku niliyik kāṭṭi, utukkāṇ eṉṟāṉ. pāttūṇ tokutta āṇmic ceyalāṟ piṟanta perumita mākaliṉ, ataṉip peruntaku nili eṉṟāṉ. mēlum "viṉivayiṟ pirintōṉ mīṇṭuvarukāli, iṭiccura maruṅkil tavirtal illi1" eṉpatupaṟṟi viriviyē vitantu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. maḻiyāl nilam īramuṟṟu meṉmi yeytuṅkāl, maṇṇiṉ aṭiyil uṟiyum nāṅkūḻppuḻu mutaliyaṉa veḷippaṭuvatum, veyil avaṟṟiṟkuk kēṭu viḷivippatupaṟṟi, nāṭkāliyē avi veḷippaṭuṅkāla
māvatum aṟintu kūṟutalāl pularā īrmaṇal malirak keṇṭi nāḷiri kavara māṭṭi eṉṟāṉ. eṉpiltaṉi veyil kāyum eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟuvatu aṟika.
22. nalvēṭṭaṉār
ippeyar accuppiratiyil kāṇappaṭavilli yāyiṉum tēvar ēṭṭilum, ceṭṭiyār ēṭṭilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivar miḷikiḻāṉ nalvēṭṭaṉār eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvatu nōkkumiṭattu ivar miḷiyeṉṉum ūriṉar eṉpatu teḷivu. ivvūr cōḻanāṭṭil perumiḷi ciṟumiḷi eṉa iru kūṟāka iṭikkālattil pirintirukkiṟatu; perumiḷi iṭikkālattēyē perumuḷi yeṉa maruvi cayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻavaḷanāṭṭu miḷināṭṭup piramatēyamāṉa perumuḷi2 eṉa vaḻaṅkiyatu avvūrk kalveṭṭukkaḷāl terikiṟatu. miḷi nāṭeṉpatu cila kalveṭṭukkaḷil viḷināṭu eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. vēṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappaṭum viḷināṭu vēṟu; itu vēṟu. iccāṉṟōr miḷikiḻāṉ eṉappaṭutaliṉ ivar vēntaviaṟiyac ciṟanta pulami mikkavar eṉpatil aiyamilli. vēntar pōr ceytu aḻinta kaḷattil perumpuṇṇuṟṟu vīḻntōrip pēy toṭarumeṉṟum, cāṉṟōrkaḷ celvameṉac ciṟappittu ōtuvatu neṭumoḻi kiḷattalum kaṭumā ūrtalum aṉṟu; tammic cērntār eytum varuttam aṟintu iraṅkiyaruḷum aruḷākiya meṉ kaṇmiyē eṉṟum, periyōr tam neñciṟ koṇṭa āṇmi neṟi kaṭavār eṉṟum kūṟuvaṉa iv vēṭṭaṉāru ṭiya mētakka cālpiṉi viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. ivar pāṭiyaṉavākak kuṟun
tokiyilum cila pāṭṭukkaḷuṇṭu.
kaḷavoḻukkam pūṇṭa kātalariṭiyē talimakaṉ variviṭi vittup pirintāṉ; avaṉ kuṟitta paruva varavil varuvatāka vaṟpuṟuttic ceṉṟu ciṟitu tāḻppāṉāyiṉaṉ; ataṉāl talimakaṭku varuttam mikuntatu. tōḻi takuvaṉa 
kūṟit taliviyi āṟṟuvittirukkiyil, avaṉ mīṇṭu vantu cāṉṟōri viṭuttut tirumaṇam ceytu koḷvatu mēṟkoṇṭāṉ. ataṉi yaṟinta tōḻi talimakaṭkut terivippāḷāy, veyil veppattāl vāṭiya neṟpayirukku, uriya kālattil iṉiya maḻi peytatu pōla talimakaṉ varivoṭu vantuḷḷā ṉeṉṟu collaluṟṟāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ iccoṟkaḷil talimakaḷiṉ maṉaniliyi neṟpayirōṭu uvamittu uṇmi yuṇarttiya tiṟam kaṇṭu viyanta nalvēṭṭaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭil amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
koṭicci kākkum aṭukkaṟ pintiṉi 
muntuviḷi peruṅkuraṟ koṇṭa manti
kallāk kaṭuvaṉoṭu nalvari ēṟi
aṅki niṟiya ñemiṭik koṇṭutaṉ
tiriyaṇaṟ koṭuṅkavuḷ niṟiya mukki
vāṉpeyal naṉinta puṟatta nōṉpiyar
1kiyūṇ irukkiyiṉ tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ
vantaṉaṉ vāḻi tōḻi ulakam
kayaṅkaṇ aṟṟa pitaṟu kāli
2aḷḷili tiraṅkiya nelliṟku
naḷḷeṉ yāmattu maḻipoḻin tāṅkē.
itu, varivu malinta tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.
uri
koṭicci kākkum aṭukkal pintiṉi-kuṟamakaḷ kāval puriyum malippakkattu viḷinta paciya tiṉippuṉattiṉkaṇ; muntuviḷi peruṅkural koṇṭa manti-muṟpaṭa viḷinta periya tiṉikkatirik kavarntu ōṭiya manti; kallāk kaṭuva
ṉoṭu nalvari yēṟi-avvāṟu kavartali aṟiyāta āṇ
kuraṅkōṭu kūṭi nalla pāṟi yoṉṟiṉ mēl ēṟiyiruntu; aṅki niṟiya ñemiṭikkoṇṭu-taṉ aṅki yiṟ picintu kaṭuvaṉukku aḷittut tāṉum kikkoṇṭu; taṉ tiriyaṇal koṭuṅkavuḷ niṟiya mukki-taṉatu tiritta aṇa
liṭattu vaḷinta kavuḷniṟiya aṭakkik koṇṭu; vāṉ peyal naṉinta puṟatta-maḻinīrāl mutuku naṉintu nīrtuḷikka irukkumāṟṟāl; nōṉpiyar kiyūṇ irukkiyiṉ tōṉ ṟum nāṭaṉ-nīr mūḻkiya viratiyar annīr tuḷikka iruntu kiyil uṇavi ēnti uṇṇuvatu pōlat tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ; vantaṉaṉ-varivoṭu vantuṟṟāṉ, kāṇ; tōḻi-; vāḻi-; ulakam kayam kaṇṇaṟṟa pitaṟu kāli-ulakattil kuḷam mutaliya nīrnilikaḷ nīrvaṟṟip paciyaṟap pulartta kālattil; aḷḷili tiraṅkiya nelliṟku-kūriya ili vāṭiya neṟ payirkku; naḷḷeṉ yāmattu maḻi poḻintāṅku-naḷḷiravup pōtil maḻi poḻintāṟ pōla e.ṟu.
tōḻi, vāḻi, nelliṟku maḻi poḻintāṅku, nāṭaṉ vantaṉaṉ eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. manti kaṭuvaṉoṭu ēṟi ñemiṭikkoṇṭu, mukki, irukkiyiṉ tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ eṉa iyiyum. koṭicci, kuṟamakaḷ, aṭukkal, malippakkam. malippakkattē  yuḷḷa kāṭṭiyaḻittut tiṉippuṉam amippatu kuṉṟavar ceyalātaliṉ aṭukkaṟ pintiṉi eṉṟār. vaḷampaṭa viḷintami tōṉṟap peruṅkural eṉappaṭṭatu. muṟpaṭa viḷintu niṟkum katir paruttuk kāṇpārkku eḷitiṟ pulaṉātaliṉ manti ataṉik kavarntu celvatāyiṟṟu. āṇiṉattiṉum peṇṇuyir iḷamiyilēyē tāyminili eyta vēṇṭutaliṉ, ataṉ aṟivu viriviṟ ciṟakkumeṉa aṟika. atu paṟṟiyē mantiyiṉ kātalaṉākiya kaṭuvaṉ kallāk kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. nalla kāppuṭiya iṭam eṉṟaṟku nalvari eṉṟār. tiṉi, kampu, cāmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ katirkaḷip picintu tuyyiṉippōkki maṇikaḷik koḷḷum ceyal ñemiṭutal eṉappaṭum; ikkālattil atu ñemiṭṭutal eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. kuraṅkiṉ irukavuḷilum uḷḷē pakkat
tukku oṉṟāka iru pikaḷ uṇṭu; kikkuk kiṭittataṉi viriya vāyiliṭṭuk kavuṭpiyil aṭakkik koṇṭu ceṉṟu vēṟoru takka iṭattēyiruntu ciṟitu ciṟitāka meṉṟuṇpatu kuraṅkiṟkiyalpu. tiṉiviḷi kālam maḻi peyyum kālamātaliṉ, manti pōntu katir kavarntāṭiya pōtu maḻi peytami tōṉṟa vāṉpeyal naṉinta puṟatta eṉṟār. maḻi peyta pōtu kuṟamakaḷ paraṇiliruntu veḷiyē vārāḷātaliṉ, mantikkuk katirkaḷik kavartal eḷitāyiṟṟu. kaṭuvaṉum uṭaṉ pōntu maḻiyil  naṉintami viḷaṅka puṟatta eṉap paṉmiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. nīr palakāl mūḻkalum ōriṭattum nilipeṟa nillāmiyum viratiyar oḻukkamātalāl, avarkaḷ ōriṭattē iruntu koṭuppār koṭukkum uṇavik kiyē kalaṉāka ēṟṟu uṇpar; iruntuṇṇum kuraṅku, nōṉpiyar puṟattē nīr oḻuka iruntuṇpatu pōṟaliṉ, nōṉpiyar kiyūṇ irukkiyiṉ tōṉṟum eṉṟār. kaṇṇaṟutal, īramiṉṟip pulartal. pitaṟukāli, pacumiyiṉṟi vaṟṟiyularum kōṭik kālam. pulliliyātaliṉ nelliṉ ili aḷḷili eṉap
paṭṭatu. pakaṉmaḻiyiṉum irāmaḻiyāl nilam mikak kuḷirum eṉpa. ataṉāl naḷḷeṉyāmattu maḻi eṉṟār.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukum talimakaṭku aḻivil kūṭṭam peṟutaṟ poruṭṭu eḻunta peruvituppuk kaṇṭum tali makaṉatu varivu muyaṟci yaṟintum, avaṉ varivoṭu varutali mikka ārvattoṭu etirnōkki yirunta tōḻi, avaṉ varavaṟintatum virintu pōntu urikkumāṟu tōṉṟa vantaṉaṉ eṉṟum, talimakaṭku uvaki mikutaliṉ, vāḻi tōḻi eṉṟum, avaṉ varumuṉ taliviyiṉ uḷḷam vēṭki vemmiyāl īramaṟṟu vāṭiyiruntamiyum, uṇmiyuṇarvu tiraṅkic curuṅki yiruntamiyum cuṭṭi, kayam kaṇṇaṟṟa pitaṟu kāli aḷḷili tiraṅkiya nelliṟku naḷḷeṉ yāmattu maḻi poḻintāṅku eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. talivaṉ vantami yāl viḷiyum payaṉ kūṟaluṟṟa tōḻi, ataṉip peruṅkural kavarnta manti kaṭuvaṉoṭu nalvari ēṟi mukki iṉitu tōṉṟum eṉṟāḷ; nīyum maṇavāḻvu mēṟkoṇṭu tali makaṉōṭu avaṉ maṉiyakam aṭintu avaṉ tāḷāṟṟit tanta poruḷip pāttuṇṭu iṉitu vāḻvāy eṉṟu uḷḷuṟut turitta
vāṟu. 
"nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum"1 eṉṟa nūṟpāviṉ "āṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi" eṉṟa pakutikku itaṉikkāṭṭi, "itaṉuḷ tiṉiviḷi kālam vatuvik kālamāyiṉum vampamāri iṭiyiṭutalaṉṟi yāṉ kūṟiya varivu poyttaṉa rēṉum iṉṟu meyyākavē vantaṉar eṉṟāḷ" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.


23. kaṇakkāyaṉār
ivar maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭuvar. paṇṭi nāḷil nam nāṭṭil ūrtōṟum iruntu makkaṭkut tamiḻneṭuṅkaṇakkum piṟavum kaṟpittu vanta āciriyarkaḷ kaṇakkāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṇakkāyar illāta ūr naṉmi payavātu eṉpatu muṉṉiyōr mutuporuḷ. "kaṇakkāyar illāta ūrum piṇakkaṟukkum, mūttōri yillā avik kaḷaṉum-pāttuṇṇāt taṉmiyilāḷar ayaliruppum im mūṉṟum naṉmi payattal ila"2 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇka. iṉik kaṇakkāyar eṉṟa coṟporuḷi nōkkiṉ, ūravariṭiyē nilavum kaṇakku vaḻakkukaḷi ārāyntu poymmi meymmi kaṇṭu urikkum matinuṭpam uṭiya pulamiyāḷar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷātal viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kaṇakkuvaḻakkukaḷi āyntu urippavar kaṇakkāyar eṉṟal poruttamākat terikiṟatu. kaṇakkukaḷi eḻutuvōr kaṇakkar eṉappaṭuvarākaliṉ, avariṉ vēṟupaṭuttaṟku ivarkaḷ kaṇak kāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar pōlum. naṭimuṟik kaṇakkum muntu nūl vaḻakkum oruṅkaṭiyarāvatu ivaratu ciṟappiyalpu. avar
pāl tamiḻp pulamiyum naṟkuṇac cālpum cērntuviṭiṉ, atu poṉ malar nāṟṟam peṟṟāṟ pōlap potumakkaṭku mikka payaṉ viḷippatākum.
ittakiya kaṇakkāyar palar iruppavum, ippotup peyarit tamakkē ciṟappākak koṇṭa iccāṉṟōr, annāḷil mikka perumiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉā reṉpatu colla vēṇṭātatākum. ivar, maturiyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉami paṟṟi, ivari maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār eṉpatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟup pōlum; ivaratu iyaṟpeyar teriyavilli. oruvarkkuc ciṟappuppeyar tōṉṟi nāṭṭil peritum payilap paravumāyiṉ, iyaṟpeyar maṟintoḻivatu iyalpu. ataṉāl, nam kaṇakkāya ṉāratu iyaṟpeyar iṟantatu eṉak koḷvatu kuṟiyākātu. nakkīraṉāri makaṉākak koṇṭa naṟṟavamum ivarkku uṇṭu. nakkīraṉār, tam tantiyi yoppac cāṉṟōr pārāṭṭum cālpu peṟṟa pōtu, maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār eṉṟu aṟiñar urikkum ciṟappup peṟṟār.
maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār, maturi nakarattavarāyiṉum ēṉi yātum nāṭē yāvarum kēḷir eṉṟa tamiḻp peruṅkoḷki tali ciṟantavar ar kālattu vāḻnta pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉi, "aṟam kaṭippiṭitta ceṅkōl uṭaṉamar, maṟañcāyt teḻuntavalaṉuyar tiṇitōḷ, palarpukaḻ tiruviṉ pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ" eṉṟum, cēramaṉṉaṉi, "oṉṉār tēem pāḻpaṭa nūṟum, tuṉṉarum tuppiṉ veṉvēṟ poṟiyaṉ" eṉṟum, cōḻamaṉṉaṉi, "viṇporak kaḻinta tiṇpiṭi yoḷvāḷ, puṉiṟṟāṉ ṟaraviṉ iḷiyar perumakaṉ tokupōrc cōḻaṉ" eṉṟum ciṟappittup pāṭukiṉṟār. eṉiṉum, ciṟappiṉṟip potumiyilli yeṉṉum muṟimikkēṟpap pāṇṭināṭṭu araciyalpaṟṟi avaratu ciṟappuṇarvu ōrō viṭattu veḷippaṭāmal illi. pāṇṭiyaratu koṟkiyi, "maṟap
pōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum koṟkip peruntuṟi" eṉavum, pāṇṭiyar "purikeḻu ciya" maliyil vāḻum yāṉiyiṉum vēṅkaṭanāṭṭiṟ piṟanta yāṉikaḷiyē mikavuṭiyar eṉpār "vaṭavayiṉ vēṅkaṭam payanta veṇ
kōṭṭiyāṉi, maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar" eṉavum urippar. mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkariyil niṟkum malittoṭaril, pālikkāṭṭup piḷaviliruntu vaṭakku nōkkic cellum toṭari vāṉamali eyaṉṟum, teṟku nōkkic celvatip poruppu eṉṟum, vāṉa maliyi yuṭiya cēraṉi vāṉavaṉ eṉṟum, poruppiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉip poruppaṉ eṉṟum paḻantamiḻar vaḻaṅkiṉar; iraṇṭiyum oṉṟāyc cērttu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar caḵkiyamali (toṭarmali) yeṉṟaṉar: caḵkiyam ciyameṉat tamiḻāyiṟṟu. caḵkiyam celvākkup peṟṟatum, poruppu, vāṉamali yeṉṟa peyarkaḷ vaḻakkiṟantu maṟintu pōyiṉa.
makaḷiruṭiya kaṇkaḷukku vēṟpaṭiyi uvamam kūṟupavāyiṉum, ivar, orutti kaṇṇiṉ amartta pārviyi, "teṇṇīrkku ēṟṟa tiraḷkāṟ kuvaḷip peruntaki citittum amiyā paruntupaṭa, vēttamar kaṭanta veṉṟi nalvēl kurutiyoṭu tuyalvantaṉṉaniṉ, arivē yuṇkaṇ amartta nōkkē" eṉpatum, varupaṭiyit tāṅki niṟkum vīramakaṉoruvaṉ ciṟappi. "vēntuṭittāṉi muṉikeṭa neṟitara, ēntuvāḷ valattaṉ oruvaṉākit taṉ iṟantu vārāmi vilakkaliṉ, peruṅkaṭaṟku āḻi aṉiyaṉ mātō" eṉpatum kaṟpōrukku mikka iṉpam taruvaṉavākum. ivar pāṭiyaṉavāka akanāṉūṟṟilum cila pāṭṭukkaḷ uṇṭu.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukum talivaṉ taliviyi viriya varintu koḷḷa niṉiyātu kātal ciṟappatu karutik kaḷaviyē virumpi yoḻukiṉāṉ. iṭiyīṭiṉṟit talimakaṉik kūṭap peṟāmiyāl talimakaḷ varuttam mikuntu āṟṟāḷāyiṉaḷ; kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumākiya piṉpu uḷatākum iṭiyīṭillāta kūṭṭam aḻivil kūṭṭam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. taliviyiṉ vēṭki atuvātalit tōḻi talimakaṉ pāl terivippa, avaṉ kaḷavē virumpum taṉ kuṟippup pulappaṭa oḻukiṉāṉ. talivipāl uḷatāya mēṉi vēṟupāṭu avaṟku iṉitu pulap
paṭāmiyāl avaṉ ivvāṟu oḻukukiṉṟāṉ eṉakkoṇṭa tōḻi, taṉ toṭiyic ceṟiya aṇintiruttalāl, ivaḷatu uṭampu "naṉicuruṅkal veḷippaṭātāyiṟṟu; āyamakaḷiroṭu viḷiyāṭṭu mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukutalāl, mēṉi niṟavēṟupāṭum maṟivatāyiṟṟu; kāval mikuvatāl niṉṉik kāṇṭaṟku iyalāmal, kaṇṇīr corintu, paṇṭiya nalam citintu nīril naṉintu tōṉṟum neytaṟpūp pōlak kaṇkaḷē kāmakkalappuṇṭu eṉpatip piṟar aṟiyap pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa; iṉi, iṟ ceṟippu mikuvatu orutali; ātalāl  nī virintu varintu kōṭalē takkatu" eṉṟu kuṟippāy eṭutturittāḷ.
ik kūṟṟiṉkaṇ uḷḷuṟu karuttāy oṭuṅki miḷirum peṇmiyiṉ oṭpam, kaṇakkāyaṉāratu nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattit toṭṭup paṇikoḷḷavum. avar ataṉi ivvaḻakiya pāṭṭiṭit toṭuttup pāṭiyuḷḷār.
toṭipaḻi maṟittaliṉ tōḷuyn taṉavē
vaṭikoḷ1 kūḻi āyamoṭu āṭaliṉ
iṭippam2 meyyatoṉ ṟuṭittē kaṭikkoḷa
aṉṉi kākkum toṉṉalam citiyak
kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaluḻta laṉṟiyum īṇṭunīr
muttuppaṭu parappiṉ koṟki muṉṉuṟic
ciṟupā caṭiya ceppēr3 neytal
teṇṇīr malariṉ tolinta
kaṇṇē kāmam karappari yavvē4.
itu, talivi āṟṟāmi yuṇarnta tōḻi talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.
uri
toṭi paḻi maṟittaliṉ tōḷ uyntaṉa-muṉkiyil aṇinta toṭikaḷ kaḻaṉṟu ukātapaṭi ceṟikkappaṭṭiruppatāl uṭampiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya melivu maṟippuṇṭaliṉ tōḷkaḷ vāṭṭam tōṟṟuviyāvāyiṉa; vaṭikoḷ kūḻi-vaṭuvakirntu vāri muṭikkappaṭum kūntali yuṭiya; āyamoṭu āṭaliṉ-āyamakaḷiruṭaṉ viḷiyāṭṭu virup
puṭiyāḷ  pōṉṟu oḻakutalāl; meyyatu iṭippum oṉṟu uṭittu-meyyiṭattē yuṇṭākiya vāṭṭamum puṟattē tōṉṟāvāṟu maṟikkappaṭum ētu oṉṟiṉi uṭiya
tāyiṟṟu; kaṭikkoḷa-kāvalmikutalāl; aṉṉi kākkum toṉṉalam citiya-aṉṉiyāvāḷ piḷḷip paruva mutalē kāttup puṟantanta paḻiya nalam keṭavē; kāṇ
ṭoṟum kaluḻta laṉṟiyum-ataṉik kāṇuntōṟum kaṇṇīr corintu aḻuvatē yaṉṟi; īṇṭu nīr muttuppaṭu parappiṉ koṟki muṉṟuṟi-nīr niṟinta muttuṇṭākum kaṭaṟparappiyuṭiya koṟkinakar muṉṟuṟiyiṉ kaṇ; ciṟu pācaṭiya ceppēr neytal-ciṟu ciṟu ilikaḷiyuṭiya pūcceppup pōlum neytaliṉuṭiya; teṇṇīr malariṉ-teḷinta nīril naṉinta malarip pōl; tolinta kaṇ kāmam karappu ariya-aḻakiḻanta kaṇkaḷ niṉṉōṭu uṇṭākiya kātaluṟaviyum maṟintu uyvikkum tiṟamiṉṟip pacantu palar aṟiyat tūṟṟukiṉṟaṉa, e.ṟu.
eṉṟatu, iṉi, niṉṉāl varintu koḷḷappaṭuvati yaṉṟi vēṟē uytiyillēm, kāṇ eṉṟāḷām. tōḷ uyntaṉa; iṭippum oṉṟu uṭittu; kaṇkaḷ kaluḻtalē yaṉṟik kāmam karappu ariya eṉa iyittuk kūṭṭi viṉi
muṭivu ceyka. paḻi, kuṟṟam, itukāṟum taṉakkum taṉṉi īṉṟu puṟantantōrkkum uḷḷa aṉpukkuriyatāy irunta uḷḷam talimakaṟkē ciṟappiṭam tantu avaṉpālē niṉivu muṟṟum celuttip pēṇat talippaṭṭamiyiṉ, kaḷaviṉ kaṇ taliviyuṭampu uṇṭiyiṟ curuṅki melivu tōṉṟiyatu kuṟṟamām eṉka. vaṭi, kūntal vaṭu, kūḻi, kūntal, iṭippu, uṭaliṭat tuṇṭākum melivu; uṭal ciṟitu melintārik kāṇpōr iṭintu pōṉār eṉpatu iṉṟum uḷḷa vaḻakku. oṉṟu, maṟittaṟkuriya ētu. kaṭi, kāval. tāṉ īṉṟu puṟantarum makkaḷatu uṭal nalamē pēṇi ataṟkuc cōr
vuṇṭākātu kāppatē aṉṉiyiṉ tāymippaṇiyāvatu paṟṟi, makkaḷ mēṉinalam tāy ōmpu āynalam eṉappaṭukiṟatu; "pāl niṉintu ūṭṭum tāy" eṉa māṇikka
vācakar kūṟuvatu īṇṭu niṉivu kūraṟ pālatu. kaṇkaḷ kaluḻtalēyaṉṟik kātaṟkalappiyum karattal ariyavāyiṉa eṉa iyikka. eccavummi tokkatu. kaṭaliṭattu eṅkum paṭātu oru cila pakutikaḷil māttiram paṭuvatāl, koṟkik kaṭaṟkaṟiyi muttup paṭu parappu eṉṟār. ceppu, pūkkaḷiṉ arumpukaḷi malara vikkum ceppuvaḷḷam; itu cempiṉāl ceyyappaṭutaliṉ ceppāyiṟṟu. acceppup pōṉṟa vaṭivuṭimiyiṉ, neytaṟpūvic ceppēr neytal eṉṟār; "ceppuvāy aviḻnta tēmpoti naṟuviri nalamalar1" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka. tōḷum meyyum nīkkaliṉ, kaṇṇē eṉṟa viṭattu ēkāram piri
nili.
varivukaṭāvum karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kūṟuvāḷāyc ciṟippuṟam niṉṟa talivaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu kūṟutalāl, tōḻi, toṭinekiḻntu ōṭumāyiṉ tōḷiṉ melivu veḷippaṭṭu maṟipulappaṭutti iṟceṟippi viḷivikkum eṉpāḷ, toṭi paḻimaṟittaliṉ tōḷ uyntaṉa eṉṟāḷ. tōḷ paḻiyuyntaṉa eṉka. niṉ mēṉikkaṇ cōrvu tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu; āyiṉum ataṉi nī āya makaḷiroṭu ceṉṟu ayarum viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ maṟittamiyiṉ, aṉṉi mutalāyiṉār aṟintu ārāytaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉpāḷ iṭippum meyyatu oṉṟu uṭittu eṉṟum, āyamōṭu āṭaliṉ atutāṉum maṟintatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. nāḷum niṉ nalamē nōkki ataṉ vaḷarcciyē karutippēṇi yoḻukupavaḷ aṉṉi yeṉṟum, aḻivil kūṭṭam paṟṟiya vēṭki niṉ uḷḷatti alittalāl piṟakkum varuttam annalattic citik
kiṉṟatu eṉṟum, ataṉik kaṇṭu niṉ kaṇkaḷ peruntuyar koṇṭu nīr coriyā niṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟip paṉmuṟiyum tuṭittu maṟikkiṉum, kātaṟ pacapput tōṉṟi veḷippaṭuttuvatu iṟceṟippum kāvaṉ mikutiyum payantu, nam kaḷaviṟku iṭiyūṟu tōṟṟuvikkum eṉa añcukiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ. aṉṉi kākkum toṉṉalam citiyak kāṇ ṭoṟum kaluḻtalaṉṟiyum eṉavum, neytal teṇṇīr malariṉ tolinta, kaṇṇē kāmam karappu ariya eṉavum urittāḷ. itaṉāṟ payaṉ talimakaṉ teruṇṭu vari
vāṉāvatu.
24. kuṉṟūr kiḻār makaṉār kaṇṇattaṉār
ic cāṉṟōrkkut tanti cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ mēlip pakuti yiluḷḷa ūrkaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉic cārntavar. avar kuṉṟūr kiḻār eṉappaṭutalāl, cōḻavēntar aviyaṟiya vāḻntu ciṟappup peṟṟavar eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. tantiyar oppar makkaḷ eṉpataṟkēṟpak kaṇṇattaṉārum annāḷil nallicic cāṉṟōrāyc ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. cōḻanāṭṭuk kāviri vaṭakarip pakutiyil pōnti yeṉṉum ūrkkuriyaṉāy neṭuvēḷ ātaṉ eṉpāṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ. "paṭuvaṇṭārkkum paṉmalark kāviṉ neṭuvēḷātaṉ" eṉa, avaṉiyum 
avaṉatu ūriyum kaṇṇattaṉār pārāṭṭip pāṭiyuḷḷār. ippōntiyi miṟikkūṟṟattup pōnti1 eṉṟu kalveṭṭukkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. mīyaṟikkūṟṟam eṉpatu iṭikkālattil miṟikkūṟṟam eṉa maruviṟṟu. muṭivēntar makaḷ kōṭaṟkuriya vēḷiratu maṟamāṇpi yuṇarttum vakiyāl tirumaṇac cevvi eytiya makaḷoruttiyik kaṇṭu ivaḷik kēṭpōr koṟṟa vēntarāyiṉum, tanti taṉṉi vaṇaṅkārkkallatu īyāṉ eṉṟu ivar pāṭuvatu mikka iṉpam taruvatu; ivaratu pāṭṭoṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟilum uḷatu.
kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭa poṟpuṭiya talimakkaḷ vāḻvil talimakaṉ poruḷ kuṟittup piriyavēṇṭiya kaṭami yuṭiya ṉāṉāṉ. kātaliyiṉ kātal mikutiavaṉatu kaṭami yuḷḷatti alittatu. poruḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi nōkkit talivaṉ tōḻipāl taṉ karuttik kuṟippāy urittāṉ. aṟaṅkaṭip paṭāta vāḻvum, piṟaṉkaṭic cellāta celvamum kātal vāḻvukku araṇum ākkamumātali yuṇarnta tōḻi, avaṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭut talimakaḷ uḷḷaṅ koḷḷumāṟu urittāḷ. atu kēṭṭatum talivi, "tam celavik kātalar kūṟiyatu kēṭṭu uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉi; ataṉāl nī naṉṟē ceytāyākiṉṟāy; āṭavar eppōtum viṉiyum poruḷum vēṇṭiyiruppar; avi kuṟittu avar pirintēkuvar; avviṉiyum poruḷum nammi  avariṟ pirikkum vaṉmiyuṭiya" 
eṉṟāḷ.
avaḷuṭiya ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, kaṟpuṭiya naṉmakaṭkuriya vāḻkkit tuṇiyām māṇpu mikkuṟṟut tikaḻvatu kaṇṭu viyanta kaṇṇattaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṭi amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
pārpaka  vīḻnta1 vēruṭi viḻukkōṭṭu
2uṭumpupuṟat taṉṉa neṭumpori viḷaviṉ
āṭṭoḻi pantiṉ kōṭṭumūk kiṟupu
kampalat taṉṉa pimpayirt tāam
3veḷḷil valci vēṟṟunāṭ ṭāriṭic
cēṟum nāmeṉac colla 4nēriḻi
5naṉṟeṉap purintōy naṉṟucey taṉiyē
ceyalpaṭu maṉattar ceyporuṭku
6akalpa āṭavar atuvataṉ paṇpē.
itu 7pirivukku uṭaṉpaṭṭa tōḻiyit talivi kūṟiyatu.
uri
pārpaka vīḻnta  vēruṭi viḻukkōṭṭu-nilam piḷavu paṭak kīḻcceṉṟa vērkaḷiyaṭiya periya kiḷikaḷiyum; uṭumpu puṟattaṉṉa neṭum pori viḷaviṉ-uṭumpiṉ puṟattōl pōla neṭiya cetilkaḷiyumuṭiya viḷā
marattiṉiṉṟu; āṭṭu oḻi pantiṉ-āṭutal oḻinta pantupōla; kōṭṭu mūkkiṟupu-kompil oṭṭiya kāmpaṟṟu; kampalattaṉṉa pimpayir tāam-kampalatti virittāṟ
pōṉṟa pacumiyāṉa payir niṉṟa vayalilē utirntu parantu kiṭakkum; veḷḷil valci vēṟṟu nāṭṭu āriṭi-viḷām paḻaṅkaḷiyē uṇavākak koḷḷum vēṟṟu nāṭṭu ariya vaḻiyiṉ kaṇ; nām cēṟum eṉac colla-yām cellukiṉṟōm eṉṟu coṉṉārāka; nēriḻi-nēriya iḻiyaṇintavaḷākiya tōḻi; naṉṟu eṉap purintōy-nalla
teṉa virumpic celavuṭaṉpaṭṭāy; naṉṟu ceytaṉi-naṟceyalē ceytāy; āṭavar ceyalpaṭu maṉattar-āṭavarkaḷ viṉi ceytaliyē niṉiyum maṉamuṭiyarātalāl; ceyporuṭku akalpa-ceytaṟkuriya poruḷ karutic celvar; atu ataṉ paṇpu-accelavu poruḷ karutum uḷḷattiṉ paṇpākum, kāṇ e.ṟu.
cēṟum nām eṉac colla, purintōy, naṉṟu ceytaṉi; āṭavar maṉattarākaliṉ akalpa, atu ataṉ paṇpu eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. viḷāmarattiṉ mēṟ
paṭṭi uṭumpiṉ puṟantōlmēṟ kāṇappaṭum cetilkaḷip pōlap porintiruppatu paṟṟi uṭumpu puṟattaṉṉa neṭum pori viḷavu eṉṟār. porintatu pōṟaliṉ pori eṉappaṭṭatu. paccippacēreṉat tōṉṟum payir niṟinta vayal pacuṅkampalam virittāṟ pōlum kāṭci yuṭittātaliṉ kampalat taṉṉa pimpayir eṉṟār. virinta kampalattiṉ mēl āṭātu kiṭakkum pantupōlap pacumi niṟaṅkoṇṭa payirniṉṟa vayaṟparappil viḷāmpaḻam utirntu kiṭakkum eṉpatām. veḷḷil, viḷāmpaḻam valci, uṇavu, purital, virumputal, ceyalpaṭum maṉam, ceyviṉik kuriya kālam iṭam karuvi viṉivaki mutaliyavaṟṟiyē eṇṇum maṉam. ceyviṉiyālaṉṟip peṟalākāmiyiṉ ceyporuḷ eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.
talimakaṟkut tāṉ celavuṭaṉpaṭṭatit tōḻi tali makaṭkuk kuṟippāy uṇarttalum, talimakaḷ varuntuvaḷō eṉa añci niṉṟāṭku, avaḷ, āṇmi tōṉṟa viṉi mēṟ koṇṭu poruḷ ceyvatē uyirāy aminta āṭavar ceyalātaliṉ, atuvē eppōḻtum niṉiyum maṉamuṭiyar avar eṉpāḷ, ceyalpaṭu maṉattar eṉṟum, atu kuṟittavaḻip piṟa yātum poruḷāka matiyār eṉpāḷ ceyporuṭku akalpa āṭavar eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. "viṉiyē āṭavarkku uyir"1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. illiruntu makiḻpavarkkup poruḷum pukaḻum illi
yātaliṉ pirintoḻukutal poruḷmēṟ cellum uḷḷattiṟkup paṇpu eṉpāḷ atu ataṉ paṇpu eṉṟāḷ. aḵtu eṉṟu niṟṟaṟkuriya āytam vikārattāl tokkatu. "uṇṇāmi vēṇṭum pulāal maṟṟoṉṟiṉ puṇṇa tuṇarvārp peṟiṉ"2 eṉṟāṟ pōla. ataṉi naṉku tērntu nī pirivuṭaṉ paṭṭatu naṉṟē eṉpāḷ naṉṟu eṉap purintōy naṉṟu ceytaṉi eṉṟāḷ. iṉi naṉṟu ceytaṉi eṉṟati etirmaṟik kuṟippumoḻiyākak koṇṭu, "tōḻi, nī pirivu uṭaṉpaṭṭami kūṭātu; tītu ceytaṉi eṉṟum, nāḷum poruḷ ceytaliyē karutum pāṉmiyar āṭavar eṉṟum, ataṉāl avar apporuḷ kuṟittuc cellum celaviyē virumpuvar eṉṟum, ivvāṟu avarāl virumpic ceyyappaṭum poruḷ tāṉum, yāṇṭum nillātu nīṅkum paṇpiṟṟākavum ataṉmēl uḷḷam celuttu
vārkku nampāl aṉpiṉṟip pirintēkum karuttu uṇ
ṭāvatu iyalpu; ataṉi vilakki uyirōṭu oṉṟi niṉṟu, atu celvuḻic cellum kātaliṉ māṇpik kaṭṭurittu avar celavi maṟuttiruttal vēṇṭum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ eṉa vēṟu poruḷ kōṭalum oṉṟu poruṭkuriya paṇpu ataṉi viḻiyum uḷḷattukkum āyiṟṟeṉpāḷatuvataṉ paṇpu eṉṟāḷ eṉa urikka.
"avaṉaṟivu āṟṟa aṟiyumākaliṉ" eṉṟa nūṟpāviluḷḷa "koṭumi yoḻukkam tōḻik kuriyavi, vaṭuvaṟu ciṟappiṉ kaṟpiṉ tiriyāmik kāytalum uvattalum pirit
talum peṭṭalum, āvayiṉ varūum palvēṟu niliyiṉum" eṉṟa pakutikku ippāṭṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, "itu naṉṟu ceytaṉi yeṉat talivi uvantu kūṟiyatu" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar1.


25. vaṭavaṇṇakkaṉ pēricāttaṉār
pēri cāttaṉār poṉṉiṉ ōṭṭam kāṇum vaṇikar. pēri, ālampēri eṉpaṉa vaṇikar kuṭippeyar; ikkālattum pēri eṉpatik kuṭippeyarākak koṇṭōr pēri ceṭṭikaḷ eṉat toṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. poṉṉiṉ ōṭṭam kāṇum toḻil vaṇṇakkam eṉap paṇṭināḷil vaḻaṅkiṉa
miyiṉ, ivar vaṇṇakkaṉ pēricāttaṉār eṉak kuṟikkap
paṭukiṉṟār. mēlum, ivar vaṭapulattil ittoḻil ceytami
yāl ivari vaṭavaṇṇakkaṉ eṉac ciṟappit tuḷḷaṉar. toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vaṇṇakkaṉpāṭi, vaṇṇakkaṉūr eṉa ūrkaḷ iṉṟum iruppatu itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu pakarukiṟatu. poṉṉiṉ ōṭṭam pārkkum vaṇṇakkar iṭikkālattum irunti rukkiṉṟaṉar; tiruviyāṟṟu mutal irācātirācaṉ kal voṭṭoṉṟil vaṇṇakkaṉ cāttaṉ niṉṟāṉāṉa vīra cōḻa aṇukkaṉ1 eṉa oruvaṉ kāṇappaṭuvatu itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. innūl ārāyccikkuk kiṭitta ēṭukaḷil ellām vaṭavaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatēyaṉṟi vaṭama vaṇṇak kaṉ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭāmi iṅkē kuṟikka vēṇṭi yirukkiṟatu. ippēricāttaṉār kālattē cēranāṭṭil māntarañ cēral irumpoṟiyum, cōḻa nāṭṭil irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum pāṇṭināṭṭil ilavantikip paḷḷit tuñciya naṉmāṟaṉum iruntu āṭciceytaṉar. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ ēṉit toki nūlkaḷilum uḷḷaṉa.
tamittuk kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭa talimakkaḷ tam miṭiyē tōṉṟiya kātal vaḷarntu perukutaṟku vāyilākiya kaḷavuneṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. talimakaṉ talimakaḷiṉ uyirttōḻiyatu tuṇiyip peṟa muyaṉṟu avaḷik 
kaṇṭu taṉ kātaṟkuṟippi aṟivittāṉ. taliviyiṉ karutti aṟiyāmiyāl, avaḷ talimakaḷi ārāyntu avaḷ maṉattil avaṉpāṟ koṇṭa kātaluṇmiyi yuṇarntu koṇṭāḷ. eṉiṉum, talimakaḷ ataṉit taṉakku aṟiviyāmiyāl oṉṟum aṟiyāḷpōṉṟu oḻukalāṉāḷ. tali
vaṉatu kātalaṉpit talivik kuṇartti iruvariṭiyē yuḷatākiya kātaluṟavik kaḷaviṉkaṇ vaḷarppatu muṟi yeṉpatupaṟṟit taliviyi yaṭintu ēṟṟa cevvi nōkkit talimakaṉ vēṇṭukōḷit terivikkiṉṟāḷ. talimakaḷi nōkkat tōḻi ōraḷavu niliyil vēṟupaṭṭavaḷāvaḷ. ataṉāl, āṇum peṇṇumākiya talimakkaḷ uḷḷaṅ kaḷip piṇittu niṟkum kātal neṟiyil, iruvariṉ vēṟāya tōḻi pukuntu ataṟku utavi ceytal eṉpatu mikka arumiyum nuṇmiyum poruntiyatākum. iyaṟki matinuṭpamum, ceyaṟkiyaṟivoṭpamum oruṅkē urukkoṇṭārkkaṉṟi aḵtu eḷitiṉ iyaluvataṉṟu; ciṟitu vaḻuviṉum nāṇaḻivum māṉakkēṭum payantu talimakkaḷ vāḻvukkē iṟuti viḷi
vittuviṭum. nuṭpamum tiṭpamum poruntiya maṉattiṉaḷākiya tōḻi, acceyali mēṟkoṇṭu, talimakaḷōṭu uriyāṭaluṟṟu. "tōḻi, nām nēṟṟup puṉattē kiḷi kaṭintu koṇṭirunta kāli namakkut tuṇiyāy akkiḷikaḷik kaṭintāṉaṉṟō oru kāḷi; avaṉ appōḻtu nammōṭu uriyāṭuvāṉ peritum muyaṉṟāṉ; nāṉō ciṟitum iṭantaravilli; avaṉum oṉṟum uriyiṉṟi veṟitē ceṉ
ṟoḻintāṉ; avaṉ akkāli eytiya varuttattip paṟṟi
yaṉṟu yāṉ ippōtu colleṭuppatu; avaṉ ceṉṟapōtu eṉṉip pārtta pārvi eṉ toṭi kaḻaṉṟu tāṉē ukum niliyi eṉakku uṇṭākkiṟṟu; atu piṟarkkut teriyātavāṟu toṭiyic ceṟittuk koṇṭēṉ; avaṉ tēmpū ituveṉat tērntu ūtāta vaṇṭu pōlpavaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ poruṭṭu yāṉ toṭi ceṟittuk koṇṭa paṇpil ceyki niṉintu eṉ neñcam varuntukiṟatu, kāṇ" eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ pulappaṭum tōḻiyiṉ matinuṭpamum aṟivoṭpamum pēricāttaṉār uḷḷattil viyapput tōṟṟuvikkavē, avar avaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟi ippāṭṭiṉkaṭ peytu pāṭukiṉṟār.
avvaḷi veriniṉ arakkīrt taṉṉa
cevvari itaḻa cēṇnāṟu 1piṭaviṉ
naṟuntā tāṭiya tumpi pacuṅkēḻp
poṉṉuri kalliṉ naṉṉiṟam peṟūum
2vaḷamali veṟpaṉ nerunal nammoṭu
kiḷimali ciṟutiṉik kiḷikaṭit taciic
3colliṭam peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ 4peyarnta
allal aṉṟatu kātalan tōḻi
tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṉ pōtuterin tūtā
vaṇṭō raṉṉaavaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci
kaṇṭum kaḻaltoṭi valitteṉ
5paṇpil ceyti 6niṉippā kiṉṟē
itu, talimakaṉit tōḻi kuṟipayappak kūṟiyatu.
uri
avvaḷi veriniṉ arakku īrttaṉṉa-aḻakiya vaḷiyiṉatu mutukil cevvarakkiṉāl varikaḷit tīṭṭiṉāṟ pōṉṟa; cevvari itaḻa cēṇnāṟu piṭaviṉ-civanta vari paranta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya piṭaviṉ neṭun tūrattiṟ parantu maṇam kamaḻum pūviṉuṭiya; naṟuntātu āṭiya tumpi-naṟiya tātiṉip paṭintuṇṭa tumpi; pacuṅkēḻ poṉṉuri kalliṉ naṉṉiṟam peṟūum-pacumi niṟam poruntiya poṉṉurikkappaṭṭa kalliṉatu niṟatti yaṭiyum; vaḷamali veṟpaṉ-vaḷaviya malināṭaṉ; nerunal-nēṟṟu. nammoṭu-nammōṭē yiruntu tuṇipuriyumāṟṟāl; kiḷi
mali ciṟutiṉikkiḷi kaṭintu acii-kiḷitta katiriṭattē niṟinta ciṟu tiṉimaṇikaḷip paṭintu uṇṇum kiḷikaḷi yōppiyiruntu; colliṭam peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ-nampāṟ collāṭutaṟku ēṟṟa iṭam kiṭikkap peṟāmiyāl nīṅkiṉāṉ; atu peyarnta allal aṉṟu-ippōtu yāṉ kūṟumatu avaṉ ceṉṟataṉāl uṇṭākiya varuttam aṉṟu; kātalam tōḻi-kātalaṉpuṭiya tōḻiyē; tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṉ-tātiṉi yuṉṉum viruppattāl; pōtu terintu ūtā vaṇṭōraṉṉa -ūtutaṟku uriyatu uriyatallātatu eṉap pūkkaḷit terintuṇarntu ūtāta kaḷivaṇṭip pōlum; avaṉ taṇṭākkāṭci-avaṉatu amiyāta tōṟṟatti; kaṇṭum-kaṇṭaṟintum; kaḻal toṭi valitta eṉ paṇpil ceyti niṉippākiṉṟu-kaḻaṉṟōṭum toṭiyic ceṟittuk koṇṭa eṉ naṟpaṇpiṉpāṟpaṭāta ceyki eṉ neñciṉ niṉippāka irukkiṉṟatu; ītu ōr allal iruntavāṟu eṉ e.ṟu.
tōḻi, veṟpaṉ nerunal nammoṭu kiḷikaṭintu acii, peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ; atu peyarnta allal aṉṟu; avaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci kaṇṭum valitta eṉ paṇpil ceyti niṉippākiṉṟu eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. aṉṟatu eṉṟaviṭattu atu eṉpatip pakutip poruḷatākak kōṭalum oṉṟu. peyarnta atu allal aṉṟu eṉ paṇpil ceyti allal tarum niṉippākiṉṟu eṉa māṟi iyittu muṭittalum oṉṟu. veḷḷiya piṭavam
pūviṉ akavitaḻ civanta narampu ōṭiṉāṟ pōla vari porunti
yiruppatu, caṅkiṉ mutukil cevvarakkik koṇṭu kōṭiṭṭāṟpōla vuḷatu eṉṟaṟku avvaḷi veriniṉ arakku īrttaṉṉa eṉṟār. neṭuntūrattē naṟumaṇam kamaḻum māṇpu paṟṟic cēṇāṟu piṭavu eṉappaṭṭatu. piṭavu, piṭāmaram. kariya niṟam paṭitta tumpi piṭaviṉatu poṉṉiṟam koṇṭa tātu paṭintu tōṉṟuvatu poṉṉuritta kaṭṭaḷik kaṟpōla vuḷatu eṉpār, poṉṉuri kalliṉ nalniṟam peṟūum eṉṟār. acii, taṅki. colliṭam, collāṭaṟku ēṟṟa iṭamum kālamum vāynta iṭam. vēṭki mikkaviṭattuc ceyvatum tavirvatum terintu ceyyum aṟivu cōrntoḻitaliṉ, tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṉ eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu.  taṇṭāk kāṭci, amiti peṟātu alamarum uḷḷattāṟ piṟakkum taṭumāṟṟat tōṟṟam. kaḻal toṭi, tāṉē kaḻaṉṟu ōṭi yukum toṭi  paṇpu. pāṭaṟin  toḻukum ceyaṟpaṇpu. niṉippākiṉṟu, niṉiviṉkaṇ koṇṭuḷatu.
tāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟic ceviyēṟṟut talimakaḷ iṇaṅkumāṟu avaḷ maṉaniṉivit taṉṉōṭu oruppaṭukkum karuttiṉaḷātalāl nammoṭu eṉa uḷappaṭut
tiṉāḷ. nampāl kuṟiyirappāṉpōl tōṉṟi ataṟkēṟṟa cevvi eytāmiyiṉ, āṟṟātu peyarntāṉ eṉpāḷ colliṭam peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ eṉṟāḷ. atukēṭṭa talimakaḷ, ivvāṟu oḻukupavaṉ taṉ kātalaṉ eṉat teruṇṭu, āṟṟātu peyarntāṉ eṉṟa collik kēṭṭatum uḷḷam pētuṟavu eytiṉāḷ. ataṉik kuṟippāy uṇarnta tōḻi, ataṉi vilakkum karuttāl peyarnta allal aṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, talivi piṟitumōr allal avaṟku uṟṟatu pōlum eṉak kiyaṟavu paṭṭuk kalaṅkit tōḻiyi nōkkiṉāḷ. annōkkam, taliviyatu kātal mikutiyip pulappaṭuttavē, avaṉpāl parattimipaṭak kūṟaluṟṟu, yāṉ ōr ēvalmakaḷ eṉat teriyātu eṉṉik kātaṟ kuṟippōṭu nōkkiṉāṉ eṉap poyyoṉṟu paṭittut tātu uṇ vēṭkiyiṉ pōtu terintu ūtā vaṇṭōraṉṉa avaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci eṉṟāḷ; vaṇṭu pōtu terintu ūtāmikkut tātuṇ vēṭki. ētuvāyiṉāṟ pōla, taṉṉuḷḷattuk kātal mikutiyāl eṉpāl taṉ kātaṟ kuṟippik kāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟāḷāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl talimakkaḷiṭiyē muṉṉuṟavuṇṭu eṉpatit tāṉ uṇarntu koṇṭatiyum, tōḻi kuṟippāy urittāḷumāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl talimakaḷ uḷḷattil accamum nāṇamum aṭiyat tōṉṟiṉa. avaṉatu vaṇṭōraṉṉa taṇṭākkāṭci nilipēṟiṉṟit taṭumāṟuvatu kaṇṭu taṉakkum avaṉpāl aṉpu uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpāḷāy kaṇṭum kaḻal toṭi valitta eṉ ceyti eṉṟum, ataṉāl talivi mukam poṟāmiyāṟ civattal kaṇṭu, aḵtu eṉakkup pāṭaṟintoḻukāta ceyal eṉpati yuṇarkiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ eṉ paṇpil ceyti eṉṟum, annikaḻcci eṉ neñciṉkaṇ mikkuniṟṟaliṉ niṉpāl urippēṉāyiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ niṉippākiṉṟu eṉṟum urittāḷ.
"nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum"1 eṉṟa nūṟpāvil varum, "maṟintavaḷ aruka, taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum mutaṉ mūṉṟum aḷiip piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu nili
yiṉum" eṉpuḻi varum palvēṟu niliyiṉkaṇ ippāṭṭikkāṭṭi iṅkē ōtiyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭa vantatu koḷka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.


26. cāttantiyār
cāttantiyār eṉṟa ittoṭar cāttaṉ tantiyār eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu; kīraṉ tanti, kīranti eṉavum, ātaṉ tanti, ānti eṉavum paṇṭināḷil peyarkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. nāḷaṭivil kīranti ānti eṉpaṉavē makkaṭku iyaṟpeyarāy vaḻaṅkaluṟṟaṉa; avvaḻakkāṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil cāttantiyār eṉpatu iccāṉṟōrkku iyaṟpeyarāyiṟṟu. iccoṟṟoṭariṉ coṟporuḷik koḷvatāyiṉ, cāttaṉ eṉpavar ciṟanta peyarpeṟṟu makkaḷiṭiyē naṉmatippuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāreṉṟum, avarukkut tantiyākum ciṟappaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟum iṉmiyiṉ cāttaṉ tanti eṉa niṉṟu cāttanti eṉat tirintu tamatu iyaṟpeyariyum iḻantiruntār eṉṟum uṇara vēṇṭiya nilimi tōṉṟi, nallicic cāṉṟōriṭiyē oruvareṉat tikaḻum ivaratu cālpukku māṟāka niṟkumōr peyarāyviṭum.
cāttantiyār cōḻaṉ pōravik kōpperunaṟ kiḷḷiyiṉ kālattavar. cirāppaḷḷi vaṭṭattil kāviriyiṉ vaṭakariyil āmūr eṉṉum ūroṉṟuṇṭu; ataṉi mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūr eṉpatu vaḻakku. avvūril perunaṟ
kiḷḷi kālattil mallaṉ oruvaṉ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. peru
naṟkiḷḷi iḷiyaṉāyiṉum ammallaṉoṭu maṟpōr ceytu avaṉatu pīṭaḻittu veṟṟipeṟṟāṉ. ammaṟpōri nēriṟkaṇṭu viyantōruḷ ic cāttantiyārum oruvar. akkiḷḷi orukāl karantippōr ceyya nērntapōtu avaṉ viṭutta nīṇmoḻi, pulavarpāṭum poruṭ porutta muṭittātal kaṇṭa cāttantiyār iṉiya pāṭṭoṉṟil amittup pāṭiṉār. niṟka. ivarit tiṇimoḻi aimpatu eṉṉum nūlic ceyta kaṇṇañ cēntaṉārukkut tanti eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; kaṇṇañ cēntaṉār eṉṟa peyar, kaṇṇaṉ makaṉārāṉa cēntaṉār eṉṟa karuttiyuṭiyatāy avarkaḷ kūṟṟi maṟukkiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ kūṟuvatum uṇmiyeṉappaṭumāyiṉ iccāt tantiyārkkuk kaṇṇaṉā
reṉṟum peyaruṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷalvēṇṭum. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ puṟa nāṉūṟṟilum uṇṭu. 
kaṟpuṭiya maṉiyaṟam purintoḻukum talimakaṉukku maṉiviyiṟ pirintu cellavēṇṭiya kaṭamiyoṉṟu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉi avaṉ talimakaṭkuk kuṟippāy uṇarttiṉāṉ. pirivāṟṟātu avaḷ mēṉi vēṟupaṭalāṉāḷ. pirintu cēṟaṟku muṉṉamē ittuṇi varuttam eytupavaḷ meyyāṟ pirintavaḻi āṟṟuvatu eṅṅaṉam eṉat tōḻi karuti neñcam añci avaṉi nēriṟ kaṇṭu celavu aḻuṅkuvikkum nōkkattuṭaṉ cila kūṟaluṟṟāḷ. piriviṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyi avaṉ naṉku eṭuttōtiṉāṉ. atu kēṭṭa tōḻi, niṉittāl cila nāṭkaḷukku avaṉ taṉ pirivit taḷḷivikkalām eṉpati aṟintāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ avaṉi nōkki, "peruma, kaḷaviṉkaṇ ivaḷuṭiya tamar varivumaṟutta pōtu nī uṭaṉpōkkiṉit tuṇintāy; tāymaṉi malipōṉṟa neṟkūṭu niṟintu peruñcelvam koḻikkum perumaṉiyātali niṉiyātu, veyil eṟikkum kaṭuñcurattiṉ vemmiyiyum karutātu niṉpāṟ koṇṭa peruṅkātalāl niṉakkuk keṭutuṇiyāy eṉ tōḻiyākiya talimakaḷ vantāḷaṉṟō? ataṉi niṉikkumpōtu aḵtu avaḷatu tōḷ ceyta tavaṟō eṉa eṉ neñcam niṉikkiṉṟatu; niṉ pirivuk kuṟipput terinta māttiriyē ivaḷ tōḷ melintāḷ; vaḷikaḷ nekiḻntaṉa; inniliyik kāṇa eṉ maṉam varuntukiṟatu, kāṇ" eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, celavaḻuṅkuvikkum tōḻipāl paṇṭu nikaḻnta kātaṟceyali niṉippittut talivaṉatu kalaṅkāvuḷḷattik kalakkum cūḻcci nuṇṇitiṉ amin tiruppatu kaṇṭa cāttantiyār ataṉi ip pāṭṭiṉ kaṇ tokuttup pāṭukiṉṟār. 
nōkō yāṉē nekiḻntaṉa vaḷiyē
1cevval cērnta 2puḷḷi veḷḷari
viṇṭup puriyum puṇarnili neṭuṅkūṭṭup
piṇṭa nelliṉ tāymaṉi yoḻiyac 
cuṭarmuḻu teṟippat tiraṅkiya3 ceḻuṅkāy
muṭamutir palaviṉ attam nummoṭu 
keṭutuṇi4 yākiya tavaṟō viyeyiṟṟup
poṉpotit taṉṉa cuṇaṅkiṉ
miṉṉī5 rōtip paṇipperun tōḷē.
itu, pirivukkuṟippu uṇarntu talivi vēṟupaṭṭami collit tōḻi celavaḻuṅkuvittatu.
uri
cevval cērnta puḷḷi veḷḷari-cemmaṇ pūcappaṭṭu niṉṟa puḷḷi poruntiya veṟṟariyiṉi yuṭiya; viṇṭup puriyum puṇarnili neṭuṅkūṭṭup piṇṭa nelliṉ-malipōla oṉṟaṉpakkal oṉṟāka aṭukkiya niliyuṭiya neṭiya neṟkūṭṭiṉkaṇ nirampiya nelliṉiyuṭiya; tāymaṉi yoḻiya-tāymaṉi taṉittoḻiya; cuṭar muḻuteṟippa-ñāyiṟṟiṉ veyil naṉku vetuppa; tiraṅkiya-vāṭiya; ceḻuṅkāy muṭamutir palaviṉ attam-ceḻumiyāṉa kāykaḷiyuṭiya vaḷintu mutirnta palā maraṅkaḷ niṟkum vaḻiyil; nummoṭu keṭutuṇiyākiya tavaṟō-keṭu tuṇiyāy nummoṭu vantataṉāl tavaṟuṭiyavāyiṉavō; vi eyiṟṟu-kūriya paṟkaḷiyum; poṉ potin
taṉṉa cuṇaṅkiṉ-poṉṉip potintu vittāṟ pōṉṟa tēmaliyum; miṉṉīrōtip paṇip peruntōḷ-oḷirkiṉṟa naṟiya kūntaliyumuṭiya ivaḷuṭiya periya tōḷ
kaḷ; melintamiyiṉ; vaḷi nekiḻntaṉa-vaḷikaḷ nillātu kaḻaṉṟu ōṭukiṉṟaṉa; yāṉ nōku-yāṉ varuntukiṟēṉ, kāṇ e.ṟu.
miṉṉīrōti, tāymaṉi yoḻiya, nummoṭu attam keṭutuṇi ākiya tavaṟō paṇipperuntōḷ vaḷi nekiḻntaṉa; yāṉ nōku eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka.  nōku, taṉmi viṉimuṟṟuttiri col. cevval, cemmaṇ. neṟkūṭukaḷiṉ aṭiyil cemmaṇṇum cāṇamum kalantu meḻukivippatu tōṉṟac cevval cērnta veḷḷari eṉṟum, veḷḷiya vikkōṟpurikaḷ neruṅkac cuṟṟip piṇittiruppatāl cemmaṇ pūcciṉ iṭiyē puḷḷikaḷ tōṉṟuvatu puḷḷiyiṭṭatu pōṟaliṉ, puḷḷi veḷḷari eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. neṟkūṭṭiṉ talipōlak kuviyātu vaṭṭamāyk kōkkālkaḷiṉ mēl niṟṟalpaṟṟi veḷḷari eṉappaṭṭatu eṉṟalumām. viṇṭu, malik kuṉṟu. "viṇṭu aṉṉa veṇṇeṟ pōrviṉ"1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. puṇarnili neṭuṅkūṭu, oṉṟuk koṉṟu nerukkamākap pakkattē puṇarttu amikkappaṭṭa neṭiya kūṭukaḷ. ikkūṭukaḷik karici yeṉṟalum vaḻakku. piṇṭam, kuviyal, cuṭar, īṇṭu ñāyiṟṟiṉ mēṟṟu. palaviṉ ceḻumiyum, veyil mikutiyāl vaṟṟit tiraṅkik kaḻintamiyum tōṉṟa
niṟkum neṭuñcurattiṉ koṭumiyic cuṭar muḻuteṟippat tiraṅkiya ceḻuṅkāy muṭamutir palaviṉ attam eṉṟār. keṭutuṇi, keṭṭārkku vēṇṭuvaṉa utavi ak kēṭṭiṉiṉṟum eṭukkum tuṇivar; keṭuṅkāli vēṇṭuvaṉa utavum tuṇi yeṉṟu mām; keṭāvāṟu kākkum tuṇi keṭutuṇi eṉappaṭṭatu. tuṇiyātal tavaṟaṉmi pulappaṭuttaliṉ ōkāram etirmaṟi. paṇi, perumi; mūṅkilumām.
talivaṉic celavaḻuṅkuvikkum karuttiṉa ḷākiya tōḻi, pirivu paṟṟiya avaṉ maṉattiṇmi kaṇṭu, paṇṭi uṭaṉpōkkiṉi avaṉ neñciṉkaṭ koṇarntu nekiḻ
vikkiṉṟāḷ. talimakaṉ uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇinta pōtu talivi ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷāka, akkāli avaḷ poruḷāka matiyātu kinekiḻttup pōnta tāy maṉiyiṉ celva niliyi viṇṭuppuriyum puṇarnili neṭuṅkūṭṭup piṇṭa nelliṉ tāymaṉi eṉa vitantu kūṟiṉāḷ; poruḷ naccip pirivatu niṉakkuk karuttāka, ataṉiṉum peṟaṟku arumiyum ciṟappumuṭiyatāka niṉ kātalik karutiṉāḷ talimakaḷ eṉat taṉ karutti vaṟpuṟuttuvatu tōḻiyiṉ cūḻcciyātal kāṇka. niṉ poruṭṭut taṉ iṉiya uyiriyum vaḻaṅkum uyarkaṟpiṉaḷ eṉpāḷ, cuṭar muḻutu eṟippat tiraṅkiya ceḻuṅkāy muṭamutir palaviṉ attam nummoṭu keṭutuṇi yāyiṉaḷ eṉṟum, ivvāṟu keṭutuṇiyākiya tōḷkaḷ ākkam peṟātu melivu eytuvatu kūṭātu eṉṟaṟkuk keṭu
tuṇiākiya tavaṟō eṉṟum, tavaṟu ceyyāmiyāṟ perumi ciṟantavi eṉpāḷ paṇip peruntōḷ eṉṟum, tavaṟu ceyyātār, atu ceytār tuṉpam eytuvatu kāṇumiṭattu avarkku atu kiyaṟavu payattal iyaṟkiyātaliṉ, yāṉ varuntā niṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ nōkō yāṉē eṉṟum, ceṟintirunta vaḷikaḷ tōḷ melivuṟṟamiyiṉ kaḻaṉṟōṭutal eṉ karutti aḻikkiṉṟatu eṉṟaṟku nekiḻntaṉa vaḷi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. inniliyil niṉ neñcamum nekiḻntu kaluḻtal vēṇṭum eṉak kuṟippāṟ cela vaḻuṅkuvittavāṟu.


27. kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār
kaḷavuk kātaloḻukkam pūṇṭa talimakaṉ, pakaliṉum iraviṉum kuṟivaḻip pōntu talimakaḷik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu nīṅkukiṟāṉ. avaṉ collum ceyalum kaḷavu nīṭṭikkum kuṟippilē niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉi yaṟinta tōḻi, avaṉ muyaṟcikku iṭiyīṭu viḷivittu viriya varintu koḷḷum muyaṟciyil avaṉ karuttic celuttukiṉṟāḷ. orunāḷ avaṉ talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē vantu niṟpatit tōḻi kaṇṭukoṇṭu taliviyoṭu collāṭupavaḷpōla avaṉ kēṭkumaḷavil niṉṟu, "tōḻi, nam kaṭaṟkarik kaḻiyarukē pūttirukkum pūkkaḷiṉ nuṇṇiya tātiṉi vaṇṭiṉam pōntu utirppatu kaṇṭu, avaṟṟi ōppi viḷiyāṭiṉōmēyaṉṟip piṟar kuṟṟam kūṟaṟkuriya ceyal yātum maṟivākac ceytatilli; orukāl maṟinta ceyaloṉṟu nikaḻntirukkumāyiṉ ataṉip piṟar yārum kaṇṭirukka muṭiyātu; avvāṟirukka, kayantōṟum neytal niṟiyap pūttiruppatu kaṇṭum, nam aṉṉi iṉṟu nammi viṭuttu avaṟṟik koytu viḷiyāṭi vammiṉ eṉak kūṟāḷāyiṉaḷ; eṉṉa karutik koṇṭu avaḷ ivvāṟu amintāḷ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. aṉṉiyō piṟarō kēṭpiṉ aiyuṟaṟ kiṭamillāmalum, talimakaṉ kēṭpiṉ, "iṟceṟippu uṇṭāyviṭṭa tākalāṉ, iṉic cāṉṟōr aṟiya maṇam ceytu kōṭalē takkatu" eṉat teruṇṭu muyalumāṟum  kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.
tōḻi nikaḻttiya ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, kaḷavuk kātal vāḻvil āṭavariṉum peṇmakkaḷē puriyaṟam teḷintu niṟkum poṟ
puḷḷam pūttu viḷaṅkuvar eṉṟa uṇmi iṉitu piṟaṅkutal kaṇṭa āciriyar kīrattaṉār, ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇ toṭuttup pāṭukiṉṟār.
nīyum yāṉum nerunaṟ pūviṉ
nuṇṭā 1tuṟikkum vaṇṭiṉam ōppi
oḻitiri varitta veṇmaṇal aṭikarik 
kaḻicūḻ kāṉal āṭiya taṉṟik
karantunām ceytatoṉ ṟilli uṇṭeṉil
parantupiṟar aṟintaṉṟum ilarē naṉṟum 
evaṉkuṟit taṉaḷkol aṉṉi kayantōṟu 
iṟavār iṉakkuru kolippac 1cuṟavār
kaḻicēr maruṅkil kaṇikkāl nīṭik
kaṇpōṟ pūttami kaṇṭum nuṇpala
ciṟupā caṭiya neytal
2kuṟumō ceṉṟeṉak kūṟā tōḷē.
itu, talivaṉ ciṟipputtāṉākat tōḻi ceṟippaṟivuṟīiyatu.
uri
oḻitiri varitta veṇmaṇal aṭikari-kariyi alittu nīṅkum alikaḷāl aḻakuṟuttappaṭṭa veṇmaṇal paranta kaṭaṟkariyiṭattu; kaḻicūḻ kāṉal-kaḻikaḷ cūḻnta kāṉaṟ cōliyil; nerunel-nēṟṟu; nīyum yāṉum; pūviṉ nuṇtātu uṟikkum vaṇṭiṉam ōppi-pūkkaḷiluḷḷa nuṇṇiya tātiṉi avaṟṟiṟ paṭiyum vaṇṭiṉam kāṟṟil utirppatu kaṇṭu avaṟṟi yōṭṭi; āṭiya taṉṟi-viḷi yāṭiya taṉṟi; nām karantu ceytatu oṉṟum illi-nām piṟar kāṇāvāṟu maṟittuc ceytatoru ceykiyum illiyē; uṇṭeṉil-maṟivākac ceyta toṉṟu uṇṭeṉiṉ ; naṉṟum parantu piṟar aṟintaṉṟum ilar-atu peritum parantu piṟar aṟiya veḷippaṭṭatum illi; aṉṉi evaṉ kuṟittaṉaḷ kol-aṉṉi yātu karutiṉaḷō, aṟiyēṉ; kayantōṟu iṟavār iṉakkuruku olippa-kuḷaṅkaṭōṟum ceṉṟu āṅkāṅku vāḻum iṟāmīṉkaḷik kavarntuṇṇum kokku nāri mutaliya kurukukaḷiṉ kūṭṭam āravārikka; cuṟavār kaḻicēr maruṅkil-cuṟāmīṉkaḷ ulavum kaḻicārnta iṭaṅ kaḷilē; kaṇikkāl nīṭik kaṇ pōl pūttami kaṇṭum-tiraṇṭa taṇṭu nīṇṭu nam kaṇṇip pōl malarntiruppatu kaṇṭum; ceṉṟu-aviyirunta iṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu; nuṇpala ciṟu pācaṭiya neytal kuṟumō eṉak kūṟātōḷ-nuṇṇiya palavākiya ciṟuciṟu paciya ilikaḷi yuṭiya neytaliṉ pūkkaḷik koyvīrkaḷāka eṉṟu colli nammic cellaviṭāḷāyiṉāḷ e.ṟu.
tōḻi, nerunal nīyum yāṉum vaṇṭiṉam ōppi āṭiya taṉṟik karantu ceytatu oṉṟum illi; uṇṭeṉil atu parantu piṟar aṟintaṉṟum ilar; kaḻicēr maruṅkil pūttami kaṇṭum ciṟu pācaṭiya neytal ceṉṟu kuṟumō eṉak kūṟāḷāyiṉamiyiṉ, aṉṉi evaṉ kuṟittaṉaḷkol eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. uriyiṭip pey
turittaṉa. iciyeccam, pūntātukaḷiṉ nuṭpam tēṟātu vaṇṭiṉam ciṟakukaḷāl at tātu kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkac citippatāl, avvaṇṭukaḷi ōppuvatu immakaḷirkku oru viḷiyāṭṭu. oru pūviṉ tātu piṟitoru pūviṉuḷ cēriṉ appū karukkoḷḷum; atuvē pūntātiṉ ariya ceyal. attakiya tātu citital naṉṟaṉṟeṉa ōppuvatu aṟaviṉiyām. putu maṇali otukki mīḷum alikaḷāl ammaṇal tūymiyum aḻakum oḷiyum peṟuvatu paṟṟi oḻitiri varitta veṇmaṇal aṭikari eṉṟār. kāṉal, kāṉaṟ cōli; ituvē viḷiyāṭṭiṭamum pakaṟkuṟiyiṭamumām eṉka oṉṟu illi eṉṟaviṭattuc ciṟappummi vikārattāl tokkatu iṟavu, iṟāmīṉ, cuṟavu, cuṟāmīṉ, veḷḷattaṉiya malar nīṭṭamākaliṉ, kaṇikkāl nīṭi eṉṟār. neytaṟpū makaḷir kaṇkaṭku uvamiyām ciṟappuṭittu; "nīṉiṟa neytaliṟ polinta uṇkaṇ" eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa. pācaṭi, pacumiyāṉa ili. pācaṭiya neytal, kuṟippup peyareccat toṭar. kuṟumō, mō muṉṉili yaci, kūṟutōḷ, ceyyuḷākaliṉ, ā,ōvāyiṟṟu.
talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉātali uṇarntu avaṉ cevippaṭumāṟu taliviyōṭu collāṭum tōḻi. muṉṉāḷ pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattē pōntu talimakaṉik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu makiḻnta nikaḻcciyi, avaṉ maṉattē niṉiyak kūṟum kuṟippāl vaṇṭiṉam ōppik kāṉal veṇmaṇal aṭikarikkaṇ viḷiyāṭiyatu kūṟiṉāḷ. ikkūṟṟup piṟar cevippaṭiṉ muṉṉāḷ viḷiyāṭiya  tiṟatti immakaḷir tammuṭ pēcik koḷkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉa ayirā toḻivar. iṉi, tamatu kaḷavoḻukkam aṉṉikkut terintuviṭṭa teṉpāḷ. evaṉ kuṟittaṉaḷ kol aṉṉiyeṉṟum, yāṉum nīyumāka nām aṉṉi ayirttaṟ kiṭaṉāka yātoṉṟum maṟittuc ceytatilli eṉpāḷ kāṉal āṭiyatu aṉṟik karantu nām ceytatu oṉṟum illi eṉṟum, attakiya karavu oṉṟu nampāl uṇṭā
yiṉ ataṉip piṟar yārum peritum nāṭi aṟinturit
taṉarō eṉiṉ atutāṉum illi eṉpāḷ, uṇṭeṉil naṉṟum parantu piṟar aṟintaṉṟum ilar eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. ivvāṟṟāl tāy aṟintamiyum, alar kūṟuvār uḷarātaliyum tōḻi talimakaṟkuk kuṟippāy uṇarttiḷāyiṟṟu. avaḷ kūṟiyatu ceviyēṟṟa taliviyum talimakaṉum aṉṉi ceyal yātām eṉa eṇṇaluṟṟārākat tōḻi talimakaḷi nōkki, 'neytal kaṇpōl pūttami kaṇṭum aṉṉi nammi nōkki nīvir ceṉṟu neytaṟpūkkaḷik koytu viḷiyāṭi vammiṉ eṉṟu nammic cella
viṭāḷāyiṉāḷ' eṉpāḷ, kaṇpōṟ pūttami kaṇṭum nuṇpala ciṟupācaṭiya neytal kuṟumō ceṉṟu eṉak kūṟātōḷē eṉṟāḷ. pūkkaḷ aḻakuṟa malarntiruppatu kāṇiṉ avaṟṟik koytu aṇintu kōṭaṟkaṇ makaḷirkku ārvamuṇṭātalum, avarkaḷi appūkkaḷik koytu aṇintu iṉpuṟumāṟu uriyavar viṭuvatum kuṟṟa
mākā; aṉṉi ataṉic ceyyātoḻintatu avaḷ neñcil karutuvatoru kuṟippu uṇṭu eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉpāḷ, ciṟupācaṭiya neytal kuṟumō ceṉṟu eṉak kūṟātōḷ eṉṟāḷ. eṉavē, iṉi, puṟattē ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭāvāṟu iṟceṟippu nikaḻum eṉṟāḷāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, iṟāmīṉi uṇṇum kurukiṉam āravārikkac cuṟāmīṉ kaḻiyiṉkaṇ ulavum eṉṟatu nam nalam kāṇum ayaṟpeṇṭir alareṭuttu urikkumāṟu talimakaṉ kuṟiyiṭam nōkkip pōkkuvaravu purikiṉṟāṉ eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē varivu niṉintilaṉ eṉat tuṉiyuṟukiḷaviyāl tōḻi uḷḷuṟut turittāḷāyiṟṟu.


28. mutukūttaṉār
iccāṉṟōr peyar mutukūṟṟaṉār eṉavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. uṟiyūr mutukūttaṉār eṉavum ivar kuṟikkappaṭuvatāl, ivar cōḻanāṭṭu nallicic cāṉṟōrkaḷil oruvar eṉpatu teḷivām. cōḻanāṭṭu vīri vēṇmāṉ veḷiyaṉ tittaṉiyum tiṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉiyum ivar tamatu pāṭṭukkaḷil kuṟikkiṉṟār. tittaṉatu vīri, kāviriyiṉ vaṭa kariyil tiruppaḻaṉam mutaliya ūrkaḷit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭa vīrik kūṟṟattukkut talinakar. tiruppaḻaṉattuk kalveṭṭukaḷ ataṉi vīrik kūṟṟattut tiruppaḻaṉam eṉavum, 'vikkirama cōḻa vaḷanāṭṭu vīrikkūṟṟam1 eṉavum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. vīrik kūṟṟattut tittaṉic cōḻaṉ pōravik kōp perunaṟ kiḷḷikkut tanti yeṉṟalum uṇṭu. itaṟkēṟpavē, ippakutiyil iṭikkālattil cōḻar kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya talivaṉ oruvaṉ, 'cōḻap perumāṉaṭikaḷ perunaṟkiḷḷic cōḻaṉ'2 eṉṟu tiruppaḻaṉattuk kalveṭṭukaḷil ciṟappikkap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl ikkūṟṟattu vīriyē vēṇmāṉ veḷiyaṉ tittaṉuṭiya vīriyeṉat tuṇitaṟku iṭa
muṇṭākiṟatu. mutukūttaṉār uṟiyūriṉarātalāl, atu cōḻar āṭciyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum tiṟaṅkaṇṭu viyantu, "veṉṟeṟi muraciṉ viṟaṟpōrc cōḻar iṉkaṭuṅkaḷḷiṉ uṟanti" yeṉṟu pārāṭṭi, āṅkē kāviriyāṟṟaṅ kariyil nikaḻum paṅkuṉi viḻāvip, 'paṅkuṉi muyakkam' eṉṟu iyampukiṉṟār. cīṟūrt talivaṉ oruvaṉatu ceyki nalattic "cīṟūr matavali naṉinal kūrntaṉa ṉāyiṉum, kuṟippiṉ illatu paṭikkavumvallaṉ" eṉṟum "ciṟcila variciyiṉ aḷikkavum vallaṉ" eṉṟum "uritiṉiṉ tūvavum vallaṉ tūvuṅkālē" eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṭiyiṭiyē kallā iṭiyaṉiyum, iṟpoli makaṭūuviyum, maṉṉar kaṭimukattu ukukkum paliccōṟṟiyum eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu mikka iṉpam tarukiṟatu. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ cila ēṉit toki nūlkaḷilum uṇṭu.
maṇampuṇarntu maṉiyaṟam purintu varuṅkāl, tali
makaṉ taṉ maṉiviyiṟ pirintu ceṉṟu muṭittaṟkuriya kaṭami yoṉṟuṭiyaṉāṉāṉ. talimakaḷatu kātaṟ perumi kaṇṭu avaṭkut taṉ pirivit terivippiṉ celavu aritām eṉat tērntu, piya ataṉit tōḻikku urittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ pirivāṟṟāta talivi peruvaruttamuṟṟu āṟṟāḷā
yiṉāḷ. āṟṟuvikkum kaṭamiyaḷāṉa tōḻi ettuṇiyō muyaṉṟum talivi āṟṟāmi mikku varuntutali viṭāḷākavē, tōḻi, avaḷ pakkal niṉṟu kuṇakkuṟivuṭiyaṉ eṉat talimakaṉi iyaṟpaḻikka luṟṟāl, mikukātaluṭiyaḷātalāl, appaḻippuri poṟātu āṟṟiyiruttali mēṟkoḷval eṉa eṇṇiṉāḷ. oru nāḷ, talivi taṉ talivaṉi niṉintu varunti āṟṟāmi mītūrum cevvi aṟintu, tōḻi pōntu "tōḻi, nam talivar tām pirintu celvataṟku muṉṉāḷ, eṉ kiyip paṟṟit taṉ kaṇṇil oṟṟik koṇṭu, taṉ kiyāl eṉ naṉṉutalit taṭavik koṭuttu, īṉṟa aṉṉiyē pōla iṉiya urikaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉār; eṉakkō avar karuttu iṉṉatu eṉa viḷaṅkavēyilli; avaruṭiya collum ceyalum tāṉ pirintu cellak karutiya kuṟippuṇarttuvaṉa eṉpatip piṉṉarē teḷintēṉ; iṉiya kūṟip poruḷ kavarum kaḷvaṉ pōlum koṭumiyuṭiyar kāṇ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. atu kēṭkap poṟāta talivi, taṉ āṟṟāmiyi maṟittut talivaṉ kuṟitta kālavaravu nōkki iṉitiruppāḷāyiṉaḷ.
pirivut tuṉpattukku iriyāki uḷam melintu vaṉmi kuṉṟiyirunta talivikkup poṟāmiyum maṉavaṉmiyum tōṟṟuvittaṟkut tōḻi talivaṉik koṭumi kūṟip 
paḻitta cūḻccit tiṟam kaṇṭu viyanta mutukūttaṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṭip peytu pāṭukiṉṟār.
eṉki koṇṭu taṉkaṇ oṟṟiyum
taṉki koṇṭeṉ naṉṉutal nīviyum
1aṉṉi pōla iṉiya kūṟiyum
2kaḷvaṉ pōlak koṭiyaṉ mātō
maṇiyeṉa iḻitarum aruvip poṉṉeṉa
3vēṅki tāya ōṅkumali yaṭukkattu
4aṅkaḻi nivanta piṅkaṇ mūṅkil
āṭumaḻi5 kiḻikkum ceṉṉik
kōṭuyar piṟaṅkal malikiḻa vōṉē.
itu, piriviṉkaṇ āṟṟāḷāya talivikkut tōḻi āṟṟum vaṉmi payappittatu; kuṟinayappumām.
uri
eṉki koṇṭu taṉkaṇ oṟṟiyum - eṉatu kiyip paṟṟit taṉ kaṇṇil oṟṟiyum; taṉ kikoṇṭu eṉ naṉṉutal nīviyum - taṉṉuṭiya kikaḷāl eṉatu nalla neṟṟiyit taṭavik koṭuttum; aṉṉipōla iṉiya kūṟiyum - īṉṟa tāyip pōla aṉpuṭiya iṉiya coṟkaḷi yurittum; kaḷvaṉ pōlak koṭiyaṉ mātō - kaḷvaṉip pōlac colvēṟu ceyalvēṟupaṭum koṭiyaṉāyirā niṉṟāṉ; maṇiyeṉa aruvi iḻitarum - maṇipōlat teḷinta nīriyuṭiya aruvikaḷ vīḻum; poṉṉeṉa vēṅki tāya - poṉṉeṉṉumāṟu vēṅkiyiṉ pūkkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum; ōṅkumali aṭukkattu - uyarnta malip pakkattē; aṅkaḻi nivanta piṅkaṇ mūṅkil - aḻakiya kaḻikaḷāl uyarnta paciya kaṇukkaḷiyuṭiya mūṅkil; āṭumaḻi kiḻikkum - acintēkum maḻi mēkaṅkaḷik kiḻikkum; ceṉṉik kōṭuyar piṟaṅkal mali kiḻavōṉ - ucciyiyum uyarnta muṭikaḷiyumuṭiya periya malikkuriyaṉākiya talimakaṉ e-ṟu.
maḻikiḻavōṉ, eṉ ki koṇṭu oṟṟiyum, taṉ kikoṇṭu nīviyum, iṉiya kūṟiyum pirintāṉākaliṉ, kaḷvaṉ pōlac colvēṟu ceyalvēṟupaṭum koṭiyaṉ kāṇ eṉakkūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kaṇ oṟṟuvatum, nutal nīvuvatum pirivukkuṟippukaḷ. "eṉ oṇṇutal nīvuvar kātalar maṟṟavar eṇṇuvatu evaṉkol aṟiyēṉ eṉṉum1" eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. aṉpuṭi iṉmoḻi vaḻaṅkaṟkaṇ aṉṉiyiṉ mikkōr ulakattu iṉmiyiṉ, aṉṉipōla eṉṟum, neñcil oṉṟum vāyil oṉṟumāka oḻukuvatu kaḷaveṉṉum kāraṟivāṇmi yātalpaṟṟik kaḷvaṉ pōlak koṭiyavaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. vēṅkippū poṉṉiṟatta tākaliṉ poṉṉeṉa vēṅki tāya eṉṟār. kaḻi mūṅkiliṉ irukaṇukkaḷiṉ iṭippakuti. kaṇuviṭamē paciya muḷiyum iliyum tōṉṟumiṭamātalāṉ piṅkaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi, muṭi, kōṭu, ucci, piṟaṅkal, mali.
talivaṉ pirivu niṉintu āṟṟātu varuntum talimakaḷi āṟṟuvikkum karuttiṉaḷākiya tōḻi, piriyum nāḷil avaṉ ceytavaṟṟi niṉippāḷāy, eṉ kikoṇṭu taṉkaṇ oṟṟiyum taṉki koṇṭu eṉ naṉṉutal nīviyum eṉṟāḷ, "oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻimēṉa"1 eṉṟataṉāl taliviyiṉ kiyiyum nutaliyum eṉ ki eṉavum eṉ naṉṉutal eṉavum moḻintāḷ. āṟṟuvikkum niliyil, nikaḻntatu kūṟalum ōr ētuvām eṉpati āciriyar. "nikaḻntatu kūṟi niliyalum tiṇiyē"2 eṉpar. iṉi, avaṉ colliya coṟkaḷi niṉippikkaluṟṟu avaṟṟiṭip potintu kiṭanta aṉpiṉi vitantu, aṉṉi pōla iṉiya kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu colli, ivvāṟu talivaṉ aṉpumikka collum ceyalum uṭiyaṉātal kāṭṭi muṭivil aḵtu illāṉāyp pirintāṉ eṉpāḷ. kaḷvaṉ pōlak koṭiyaṉ eṉṟāḷ. appeṟṟiyōṉ kuṉṟil maṇiyeṉa aruvi iḻitalum poṉṉeṉa vēṅki malar
talum eṉakku viyappit tarukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟaṟku maṇiyeṉa iḻitarum aruvip poṉṉeṉa vēṅki tāya ōṅkumali eṉṟāṉ. mikka nīric cumantu malimuṭivil taṅka varum maḻi mukili, mūṅkil vayiṟu kiḻittu alikkum eṉpataṉāl, taṉpāl aṉpu mikavuṭiyamāyt taṉṉiyiṉṟiyamiyē
māy aṭinta nammit taṉ pirivāl varuttukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu tōḻi koṭumi kūṟiṉāḷ. kaṇṇoṟṟiyum, nutal nīviyum, iṉiya kūṟiyum tamatu aṉpu mikutiyip pulappaṭuttiya kātalar aṉpiṉmiyum, koṭumiyum, uṭiyarallar; poruḷillārkku ivvulakam illi eṉṟa poruḷurip paṟṟic ceṉṟaṉa rākaliṉ, avar varuṅkāṟum yāṉ āṟṟi
yiruppēṉ kāṇ eṉat talivi kūṟuvāḷāvatu itaṉāṟ payaṉ eṉa uṇarka.
iṉi, tōḻi taliviyik kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatākak koḷḷiṉ, ippāṭṭuk kaḷaviṉkaṇ, talivaṉ talivi kāṭṭiya kuṟippik koṇṭu tōḻiyiyaṟintu, taṉ 
kātal taliviyāl mikkuniṟpataṉit terivittu, avaḷ naṭpiṉip peṟa muyaluṅkāl, tōḻi iruvar karuttiṉiyum uṇarntu, talivi taṉ kātali maṟittoḻukum māṇ
puṇarntu, tāṉ aṟiyat taṉ kaḷavoḻukkatti avaḷ nikaḻttuvatu naṉṟām eṉpatupaṭa, oṉṟum aṟiyāḷ pōṟ collāṭaluṟṟu, "ippakutiyil aṭikkaṭi vantu nīṅkum oruvaṉ eṉpāṟ pōntu eṉ kiyik koṇṭu taṉkaṇ oṟṟiyum taṉ kiyāl eṉ naṉṉutal nīviyum aṉṉi pōla iṉiyaṉa kūṟiyum, kaḷvaṉ pōla eṉ maṉattiyum kavarntu koṇṭu nīṅkiṉāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ eṉpatām. atu kēṭkum talimakaḷ, eṉpāl koṇṭa kātalāl itu ceytāṉāka ivaḷ avaṉi vēṟupaṭak karutukiṉṟāḷeṉa uṟṟatu kūṟik kuṟinērvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
"avaṉaṟivu āṟṟa aṟipu mākaliṉ1" eṉṟa nūṟpāvil varum, "kiḻavaṉi makaṭūup pulampu peritākaliṉ alamaral perukiya kāmattu mikutiyum" eṉpataṟku ippāṭṭiṉi eṭuttōtik kāṭṭuvar iḷampūraṇar.


29. pūtaṉār
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē peritum payila vaḻaṅkiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷil ātaṉ pūtaṉ eṉpavi ciṟantavi; āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārāl ciṟappuṟa eṭuttu ilakkaṇam kāṭṭap peṟṟavi. ivaṟṟi aṭuttu niṟkum paḻam peyarkaḷ cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ eṉpaṉavākum. ātaṉ pūtaṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ ātaṉ eṉpatu cēra nāṭṭilum, pūtaṉ eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭilum perumpāṉmiyākavuḷḷaṉa. cāṉṟōr palar iḷampūtaṉ, nappūtaṉ, veṇpūtaṉ, piḷḷippūtaṉ eṉavum, kuṉṟam pūtaṉ, cēntam pūtaṉ, cāttam pūtaṉ eṉavum palatiṟamākap peyar pūṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. paḻamiyum, potumiyum ciṟakkavuṭimippaṟṟi ivaṟṟit taṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷ eṉak kōṭal poruttamākiṟatu. pūtaṉār eṉṟa peyaruṭiyār palarum vēṟu vēṟu aṭimoḻikaḷāl ciṟappikkap peṟṟirukkavum, īṇṭu ippūtaṉār aṭipeṟātu taṉittu niṟpati nōkkiṉ, ivar ēṉic cāṉṟōr palariṉum muṟpaṭṭavar eṉṟu koḷḷalām. ivar pāṭiyaṉa innaṟṟiṇiyil māttiram kāṇappaṭuvatē im muṭivukku ōraḷavu ātaravu tarukiṟatu. pūtaṅkaṇṇaṉār, pūtam pullaṉār, pūtaṉ tēvaṉār eṉa varuvōr pūtaṉārōṭu toṭarpuṭiyar eṉpatu orutali. cēravaracar kuṭiyil ātaṉ eṉpatu peruka vaḻaṅkiyatu pōlap pūtaṉ eṉpatu, pāṇṭiya
nāṭṭu maṉṉar kuṭiyil perukak kāṇappaṭātāyiṉum, pāṇṭi vēntaruḷ teṉkumari mutal teṉveḷḷāṟu variyiṟkiṭakkum pāṇṭi nāṭṭi āṇṭavaṉāṉa pūtappāṇṭiyaṉatu peyarkkaṇ atu niṉṟu poṟpuṟukiṉṟatu.
kātalaṉpu tōṉṟi vaḷarum kaḷavuk kālattil talimakaṉ pāl kātal mikkoḻukiya makaṭ kuṟippu uṇarnta peṟṟōr avaṟku makaṭ koṭi maṟuttataṉālō, vēṟṟu varivu karutiyataṉālō, avaḷ avaṉōṭu peṟṟōr aṟiyāvāṟu uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇintu pōyviṭṭāḷ. piḷḷami niliyi
liruntē avaḷi vaḷartteṭutta cevili pirivāṟṟātu avaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyē tēṭik koṇṭu pōṉāḷ. kaṇṭōr, taṉ kātaṟ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ avaḷ makiḻntu ceṉṟa tiṟattit terivittaṉar. ataṉi accevili makaḷip peṟṟa tāykku urittāḷ. naṟṟāy taṉ makaḷ taṉakkuriya kātalaṉuṭaṉ pōkiyatu paṇṭit tamiḻneṟippaṭi aṟamēyeṉṟu tuṇintāḷāyiṉum, taṉ makaḷatu meṉmip paṇpu niṉinta pōtu avaḷatu celavu avaṭku āṟṟoṇāt tuyari viḷivittatu. "yāṉ eṉ makaḷi iṟukat taḻuvuṅkāl, avaḷatu iḷamārpu nōmeṉa añcic ciṟitu kinnekiḻvēṉāyiṉ, yāṉ pirintatāka eṇṇip perumūccuviṭṭu varuntuvaḷ; attuṇip pirivarum meṉmip paṇpuṭiyāḷ, iṉṟu pulivaḻaṅku kaṭuñcurattu neṭuvaḻiyi evvāṟu kaṭantu celvaḷ? ati niṉikkumpōtu eṉ neñcam varuntukiṟatu" eṉṟu taṉ tāymi niliyil kaṭṭurittu avalikkiṉṟāṉ.
tāyatu mikka aṉpum makaḷatu meṉmip paṇpum oruṅku viḷaṅka niṟkum māṇpu kuṟittu ikkūṟṟiṉi ippāṭṭiṭi vittu āciriyar pūtaṉār pāṭukiṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ taliviyiṉ aṉpu niliyum avaḷ pōkiya vaḻiyatu arumi niliyum oppak kāṭṭi nam aṟivip paṇi koḷḷum ivaratu pulami nalam naṉku kuṟikka takkatākum.
niṉṟa vēṉil ulatta kāntaḷ
aḻalavir nīḷiṭi niḻaliṭam 1peṟāatu
īṉṟukāṉ maṭinta piṇavuppaci kūrnteṉa
māṉṟa māli vaḻaṅkunarc cekīiya
pulipārt tuṟiyum pullatarc ciṟuneṟi
yāṅku vallunaḷkol tāṉē 2yāṉṟaṉ
vaṉintēt tiḷamuli 3nōva kolleṉa
niṉintukin nekiḻnta aṉittaṟkut tāṉṟaṉ
pēramar maḻikkaṇ īriya kaluḻa
veyya uyirkkum cāyal
miyī rōtip perumaṭat takiyē.
itu, makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.
uri
niṉṟa vēṉil ulanta kāntaḷ - niṉṟu vetuppiya vēṉilāl vāṭiya kāntaḷ; aḻal avir nīḷiṭi - neruppeṉa viḷaṅkum nīṇṭa vaḻiyil; niḻaliṭam peṟātu - niḻal poruntiya iṭam peṟāmiyāl; īṉṟu - kuṭṭiyīṉṟu iḷippaṭṭu; kāṉmaṭinta piṇavu paci kūrnteṉa - kāṭṭiṭattē maṭintu kiṭakkum peṇpuli paci mikka teṉṟu; māṉṟa māli - iruḷ mayaṅkiya mālip pōtil; vaḻaṅkunar cekīiya-vaḻiccelvōrit tākkik kollum poruṭṭu; puli pārttu uṟiyum pullatarc ciṟuneṟi - puliyēṟu avartam varavu nōkki oṭuṅkiyirukkum puṟkaḷ niṟinta vaḻiyākiya akalamillāta ciṟiya neṟiyi naṭantu cēṟaṟku; yāṅku vallunaḷ kol - evvāṟu vallami uṭiyaḷā
yiṉaḷō, aṟiyēṉ; yāṉ taṉ vaṉintu ēntu iḷamuli nōvakol eṉa niṉintu - taṉṉuṭiya toyyil eḻutiya uyarnta iḷamulikaḷ nōvumē eṉak karuti: ki nekiḻnta aṉittiṟku - taḻuviya eṉ kiyic ciṟitu nekiḻtta av
vaḷaviṟkē; taṉ pēramar maḻikkīriyaṇ kaluḻa - taṉṉuṭiya periya matarttu kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ nīr curantu coriya; veyya uyirkkum cāyal - vevvitāka uyirkkum meṉmiyum; miyīrōtip perumaṭattaki - kariya kuḷirnta kūntalum periya maṭappamum aḻakum poruntiya eṉmakaḷ e-ṟu.
maṭattaki, pullatarc ciṟuneṟi yāṅku vallunaḷkol eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. kāntaḷ aḻaleṉa avir nīḷiṭi iṭam peṟātu maṭinta piṇavu eṉka. eṉa veṉpatu eñci niṉṟatu. piṇavu paci kūrnteṉap puli cekīiya pārttu uṟiyum ciṟuneṟi, pullatarc ciṟuneṟi eṉa iyiyum, nōvakol eṉa niṉintu nekiḻnta aṉittaṟkuk kaṇ kaluḻa uyirkkum eṉṟu iyikka, cāyalum ōtiyum maṭappamum takiyu muṭiyāḷ eṉa viriyum. kāli naṇpakal māli eṉṟa muppōtum orutaṉmiyāṉa vemmiyic ceyyum vēṉil eṉṟaṟku niṉṟa vēṉil eṉṟār. māṉṟa māli eṉpuḻi, māṉṟa eṉṟatu māl eṉṉum collaṭiyākap piṟanta terinilip peyareccam. pullatar, puṟkaḷ niṟinta atar puli vaḻaṅkum atarākaliṉ, pulli mēyum māvīṉam avaṇ vārā ātalāl puṟkaḷ naṉku vaḷarntirukkumāṟu tōṉṟap pullatarc ciṟuneṟi eṉṟār. puliyō pacittālum pullit tiṉṉātu. vaṉital, toyyil eḻuti aḻakuṟuttal. aṉittu - avvaḷavu, veyya uyirttal, veytuyirttal, cāyal, meṉmi, miyīrōti, kariya neyppiṉiyuṭiya taṇṇiya kūntal. maṭam, īṇṭu iḷamik kaṇ uḷatākum maṭappam. taki, peṇmikkuriya nalam palavum nirampiyavaḻip piṟakkum aḻaku.
makaṭpōkkiya tāy avaḷit tēṭic celluṅkāl, kaṇṭōr, avaṭku vaḻiyiṉ iyalpu kūṟuvārāy, talimakaḷ ceṉṟa curam mikka veyil vemmi yuṭitteṉavum, aṅkē niḻaliṭam peṟutal ariteṉavum kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atukēṭṭu āṟṟāḷāy, niṉṟa vēṉil ulanta kāntaḷ aḻal eṉa avir nīḷiṭi niḻaliṭam peṟātu eṉṟum, avveyilil niḻaliṭam peṟātu varuntum peṇpuli, kuṭṭiyīṉṟu mēlē cellutaṟkēṟṟa valiyiṉṟi maṭintu kiṭappatōṭu paci mikuntu tuyarkiṉṟatu eṉavum, ataṉ paciyip pōkkaṟkup puliyēṟu mālippōtil avvaḻiyē varuvōrit tākkik kolvatu karuti orupāl oṭuṅkiyirukkumeṉavum avarkaḷ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, uḷḷam uṭintu kalakkam mikka cevili, avvaḻiyē eṉ makaḷ ceṉṟu cērntāḷ eṉṟu collukiṟīrkaḷē, accam ciṟitumiṉṟic cēṟaṟku avaḷ eṅṅaṉam vallavaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉpāḷ, yāṅku vallunaḷ kol eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. curattiṉ vemmiyiyum, neṟiyiṉ koṭumiyiyum, celaviṉ arumiyiyum kaṇṭōr uritta kūṟṟu avaḷ uḷḷattē accamum kalakkamum tōṟṟuvikkavum, avaṟṟi yeṇṇātu ceṉṟa talimakaḷiṉ vaṉmiyi niṉitta avaḷ neñcil, makaḷatu meṉmippaṇpē mēleḻuntu niṉṟamiyiṉ, ataṉiyē pullunekiḻnta ceyaṉmēl vittup pulampik kūṟuvāḷayiṉaḷ, puli mutaliyaṉa etirppaṭiṉ ōṭi otuṅkutaṟkuriya peruneṟi yaṉṟu eṉṟaṟkuc ciṟuneṟi eṉṟum,  puli uṟi
talāl makkaḷum mākkaḷum avvaḻiyil vaḻaṅkāmi tōṉṟap puli pārttu uṟiyum pullatar eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. vaḻiyiṉ arumiyum koṭumiyum, avaḷ neñciṭi niṉṟu varuntiṉamiyiṉ, taṉ makaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu ceṉṟa talimakaṉi niṉiyāḷāyiṉaḷ. talimakaḷ uḷḷam talimakaṟ kākātu avaḷ taṉakkē urittāy niṉṟa pōtu, ceviliyiṉ pulluc ciṟitu nekiḻiṉum āṟṟātu aḻutaṉaḷāka, ippoḻutu avaḷ uḷḷanili vēṟupaṭṭami niṉiyātu paṇṭi niliyiyē niṉintu cevili taliviyiṉ cāyal mutaliya iyalkaḷi, cāyal miyirōtip perumaṭattaki eṉṟu parinteṭutturittāḷ. kaṇṭōr curattiṉ koṭumi kūṟiyatu. ceviliyi mēlum cellāvāṟu taṭuttu maṉikkē mīḷumāṟu ceytaṟporuṭṭu.


30. koṟṟaṉār
veṟṟi eṉap poruḷpaṭum koṟṟam eṉṟa collaṭiyākap piṟantatu koṟṟaṉ eṉṟa peyar. itaṉ peṇpāl koṟṟi eṉa varum. koṟṟavi yeṉpatum ivvāṟu vantatē. ikkoṟṟaṉār, aṭimoḻi yoṉṟum iṉṟi veṟitē niṟpatu ivar mikka toṉmiyum ciṟappum uṭiyavar eṉpati uṇarttukiṟatu. iṉic cilar, cellūrk koṟṟaṉār eṉavum, cellūr kiḻār makaṉār perum pūtaṅ koṟṟaṉār eṉavum toki nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭum cāṉṟōrē ivar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; avar kūṟṟi vaṟpuṟuttum mētakka cāṉṟu oṉṟum kāṭṭappaṭavilli. maṉam pōṉavāṟu muṭivu koḷvatum ataṟkeṟpap pāṭṭukkaḷit tokuttuk kōṭalum ārāycci neṟikku aṟamākātu. ikkoṟṟaṉār tamatu peyaraḷavilēyē kaṟṟavar kūṭṭam iṉitaṟintu koḷḷum cīrtta pulami paṭittu viḷaṅkiyavareṉa amital muṟiyākum. ivar pāṭiyaṉavākak kuṟuntokiyilum cila pāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
paṇṭināḷil, iḷamakaḷir kūṭit tuṇaṅki yeṉṉum kūttiṉi yāṭi viḷiyāṭuvar. akkāli, aracarum celvarumākiya perumakkaḷ ataṟkut talikki tantu viḷiyāṭṭayarvar. orukāl, talimakaṉ, parattiyar cēriyil toṭikki makaḷirkkut talikki tantu viḷiyāṭi viṭṭut taṉ maṉikku vantāṉ. avaṉ viḷiyāṭiyatu maṉikkaṇ irunta tali
makaṭkup pulaviyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. talivaṉpāl parattami yuṇṭāyiṟṟeṉa avaḷ eṇṇiṉāṉ. avaṉō uṇmiyi yoḷiyāmal, "yāṉ yāriyum aṟiyēṉ, niṉ tōḻiyum kaṇṭiruntāḷ" eṉṟāṉ. talikki tantu viḷiyāṭuṅkāl, makaḷir avaṉip paṟṟi īrttu viḷiyāṭiyatu kaṇṭu vanta tōḻi, talivi pakkal niṉṟu, "makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭayarum teruvil, uṭikalappaṭṭōr oru palaki kiṭippa ataṉit tāntāmum cērap paṟṟuvatupōla makaḷir palar, niṉṉip paṟṟi īrppa, avariṭiyē nī kalaṅkiya niliyik kaṇṭēṉ; atu viḷiyāṭṭu marapātaliṉ yāṉ oṉṟum ceytaṟkilli" eṉṟāḷ.
tōḻiyiṉ ikkūṟṟut talivikkuc cārpāyp pulattaṟku iṭam tantu, atu tīrtaṟku vāyilākavum amintirukkum nayam kaṇṭa koṟṟaṉār, ataṉi ippāṭṭiṭi amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
kaṇṭaṉeṉ makiḻna kaṇṭevaṉ ceykō
pāṇaṉ kiyatu paṇpuṭic cīṟiyāḻ
yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ immeṉa imirum
1ērtaru teruviṉniṉ etircci nōkki
mārputalik koṇṭa māṇiḻi makaḷir
kavalē muṟṟa veytuvīḻ arippaṉi
kālē muṟṟa 1pitaṟ kālik
kaṭalmaram kaviḻnteṉak kalaṅki yuṭaṉvīḻpu
palarkoḷ palaki pōla
vāṅka vāṅkaniṉ 2ūṅkañar niliyē.
itu, parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ yāriyum aṟiyēṉ eṉṟāṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.
uri
makiḻna - talivaṉē; kaṇṭaṉeṉ kaṇṭu evaṉ ceykō - yāṉ makaḷiriṭiyē niṉṉik kaṇṭēṉāyiṉum, atu marapākaliṉ yātu ceyya vallēṉ; pāṇaṉ kiyatu - parattiyarip puṇarkkum pāṇaṉ kiyil uḷḷatākiya; paṇpuṭic cīṟiyāḻ - kuṟṟamillāta paṇpiṉiyuṭiya cīṟiyāḻ; yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ immeṉa imirum - pututtēṉ uṇṭa vaṇṭu pōla immeṉṟa iṉiya iciyic cey
kiṉṟa; teruviṉ - teruvil aminta tuṇaṅkik kaḷattiṉ kaṇ; ērtaru niṉ etircci nōkki - viḷiyāṭṭu virumpi eḻuntaruḷukiṉṟa niṉ etirppāṭu kaṇṭu niṉ talikkiyip peṟa virumpi; mārpu talikkoṇṭa māṇiḻi makaḷir - niṉ mārpiṉ māliyip paṟṟik koṇṭa māṭcimiyuṟṟa iḻikaḷi yaṇinta makaḷir; kaval ēmuṟṟa veytu vīḻ arippaṉi - kavali mikkuk kalakka muṟṟataṉāl vey
tuyirttu nutal viyarkkum tuḷiyuṭaṉ; kāl ēmuṟṟa pital kāli - kāṟṟut tiraṇṭu uyirkaḷ mayaṅki varuntutaṟ kētuvākiya puyal mōtum pōtu; maram kaṭal kaviḻnteṉa - ēṟic ceṉṟa marakkalam kaṭaliṉkaṇ kaviḻntatāka; kalaṅki - kalakkameyti; uṭaṉ vīḻpu - oruṅkēkūṭi; palar koḷ palakipōla - palarum cērap paṟṟi īrkkappaṭṭa marakkalap palakipōla; vāṅka vāṅka - avaravarum paṟṟi īrppa; niṉ - avar naṭuvē niṉatu; ūṅku añar nili - muṉṉum piṉṉumāka acintu varuntiya niliyiṉi e-ṟu.
makiḻna, pitaṟkāli, maram, kaṭal kaviḻnteṉak kalaṅki uṭaṉ vīḻpu. palar koḷ palaki pōla, yāḻ vaṇṭiṉ imirum teruvil, niṉ etircci nōkki, mārpu talik
koṇṭa makaḷir arippaṉiyuṭaṉ, vāṅka vāṅka, niṉ ūṅkañar nili kaṇṭeṉaṉ; kaṇṭu, yāṉ evaṉ ceykō eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. cīṟiyāḻkku uriya kuṇa
mellām niṟintami tōṉṟap paṇpuṭic cīṟiyāḻ eṉṟār, cīṟiyāḻ, pēriyāḻ eṉpaṉa yāḻ vaki. yāṇar, putumi; īṇṭup pututtēṉākiya varuvāy mēṟṟu, cīṟiyāḻ imirum teru, ērtaru teru eṉa iyiyum, ērtaral, 
talivaṉ eḻuntaruḷutal mēṟṟu. etircci, nērpaṭutal, talimakkaḷākiya celvar, viṟaliyar kūttiyar mutaliya āṭaṉmakaḷirkkut talikki tantu kūttu mutaliyaṉa toṭaṅkivittal paṇṭiyōr marapu. "melliṇark kaṇṇi milinta mintar, elvaḷi makaḷir talikki tarūuntu"1 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvar. ivvāṟu talivarkaḷ talikki tantu viḷiyāṭi varuvatum, aḵtu avartam maṉiyuṟi makaḷir pulattaṟku ētuvāvatum, "cilippu vallēṟṟiṉ talikki tantu nī naḷintaṉi varutal uṭaṉ
ṟaṉaḷāki, uyavum kōti ....... pēriyal arivi"2 eṉa varutalāl uṇarappaṭum tām aṇiyum iḻikaḷāṟ piṟakkum aḻakik kāṭṭi, āṭavar neñcu kavarum nīrmiyar am
makaḷir eṉṟaṟku māṇiḻi makaḷir eṉṟār. muttu muttākat tuḷikkum nīrttuḷi arippaṉi eṉappaṭṭatu. nīrāṭuvōrkkup puṇi pōla viḷiyāṭum makaḷirkkut talimakkaḷiṉ mārpum tōḷum paṟṟukkōṭām eṉka; itaṉi, "muḻavu imiḻ tuṇaṅkikkut taḻūuppuṇiyātal" eṉpa. akkaruttē tōṉṟa, īṇṭum palar koḷ palaki pōla eṉṟār. kaval, kavali. kāṟṟup periya aḷavil tiraṇṭa piṉṉarē puyalāy mōtumātaliṉ, akkāli, makkaḷum piṟavum ataṟku āṟṟātu tākkuṇṭu mayaṅkip peruntuṉpam eytuvatu paṟṟi, kāl ēmuṟṟa pitaṟ kāli eṉṟār. ēmuṟutal mayaṅkutal, pitaṟkāli, tuṉpakkālam, ūṅkunili, acintāṭum nili. añar, tuṉpam. ceykō, ceyku, taṉmi yorumi muṟṟu viṉit tiricol. ōkāram viṉā.
parattiyar tuṇaṅkikkut talikki tantu ciṟappittup pōnta talivaṉ ceyal talivi pulattaṟku ētuvāyiṉa
miyiṉ, talivi, nī parattimi pūṇṭaṉi yākaliṉ emmaṉi vāraṟka eṉa vāyil maṟuttāḷāka, talivaṉ parattiyar tuṇaṅkikkut talikki tantamiyallatu, ammakaḷiruḷ oruvariyum aṟiyēṉ eṉṟu kūṟalum, pulaviyāl mukam civantu niṟkum talivi pakkal niṉṟu pēcaluṟṟa tōḻi, makiḻnaṉē, nī tuṇaṅkikkut talikki tantu ciṟappittami yāṉ kaṇṭēṉ eṉpāḷāy, kaṇṭaṉeṉ makiḻna eṉṟum, avviṭantāṉum itu eṉpāḷ, pāṇaṉatu cīṟiyāḻ imirum teru eṉpāḷ, pāṇaṉ kiyatu paṇpuṭic cīṟiyāḻ yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ immeṉa imirum ērtaru teruviṉ eṉṟum, tuṇaṅkikkaḷam teruvātaliṉ, avaṇ avaṉ pōntamiyum, avaṉ varavit tuṇaṅki makaḷirāṉa parattiyar ārvattōṭu etirpārttiruntamiyum iṉitu tōṉṟa, ērtaru teruviṉ niṉ etircci nōkki eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. talimakaṉ talikki tantu tuṇaṅkiyit toṭaṅkiyatum, ammakaḷir avaṉ mārpum tōḷum paṟṟi āṭiṉar eṉṟaṟku mārpu talikkoṇṭa māṇiḻi makaḷir eṉṟum, āṭiya viṭattuk kāmavēṭki mītūrntu niṉ kūṭṭam peṟalākāmiyiṉ piṟanta kavaliyāl mayaṅki veytuyirttu nutal viyarttuk kaṇṇīr tuḷittaṉar eṉpāḷ, kaval ēmuṟṟa veytuvīḻ arippaṉi eṉṟum urittāḷ. kaṭuṅkāṟṟu mōta neṭuṅkaṭaluḷ kalam uṭintu keṭuvōrkku akappaṭum puṇi uyirttuṇi
yātal pōlak kāmak kaṭaluḷ vīḻntu kalaṅkañar eytum ammakaḷirkku niṉ mārpu taḻūup puṇiyāyiṟ ṟeṉpāḷ, kāl ēmuṟṟa pitaṟkālik kaṭal maram kaviḻnteṉak kalaṅki uṭaṉ vīḻpu palarkoḷ palaki pōṉṟatu eṉavum, iru
maruṅkum ammakaḷir īrttamiyāl irupālum nī acintu koṭuttu āṭiya niliyi yāṉ kaṇṭēṉ; niṉakku avarpāl kāmakkuṟippu illiyāyiṉum, ammakaḷirpāl atu mikkiruntami kaṇṭēṉ eṉavum, avaratu tuṇaṅkikku nī taḻūup puṇiyātalum, avarkaḷ niṉmārpum tōḷum talikkōṭalum niṉ talimikku māṟaṉmiyiṉ, yāṉ avaṇ ceyaṟkuriyatu yātum illi eṉavum kūṟuvāḷ, vāṅka vāṅka niṉṟu ūṅkañar nili kaṇṭaṉeṉ makiḻna, kaṇṭu, evaṉ ceykō eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷ. talimakaḷ atu kēṭṭup pulattalik kiviṭṭu nērtalē takkateṉat tuṇintu vāyil nērvāḷāvatu payaṉ.
31. nakkīraṉār
caṅkanūṟ cāṉṟōr peyar niralik kāṇpōr, nakkīrar, maturi nakkīraṉār, maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār eṉṟa peyarkaḷ iruppatik kāṇpar. ivi yāvum oruvarik kuṟippaṉavā palarik kuṟippaṉavā eṉpatil aṟiñar karuttu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. oruvariyē ivi kuṟikkumāyiṉ vēṟu vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil vēṟuvēṟu peyarkaḷik kuṟittal kūṭātu. avvappōtu vēṟu peyarkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭuvatāl, nakkīrar oruvarallar. palar eṉṟa koḷkiyē valipeṟukiṟatu. vēṟākap pakukkumiṭattu maturi nakkīraṉār oruvarākavum maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār  oruvarākavum niṟkiṉṟaṉar. iṉi, nakkīrar eṉṟa peyarē cila iṭaṅkaḷil nakkīraṉār eṉavum, nakkīrar eṉavum kāṇappaṭum paṭiṉum nakkīrar nakkīraṉār eṉṟa iraṇṭum oruvariyē kuṟikkumeṉpatil evarum vēṟu
paṭuvatilli. ākavē, caṅkac cāṉṟōruṭiya niraluṭ kāṇappaṭum nakkīrarkaḷ, maturi nakkīraṉārum maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉārum eṉa iruvarāvar eṉṟu kōṭal nēritākum.
inta maturi nakkīraṉār vāḻnta kālattil, tamiḻakattil pala iṉiya varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷ nilaviṉa. titiyaṉ eṉpāṉōṭu aṉṉi yeṉpavaṉ pakikoṇṭu avaṉatu kāval maramāṉa puṉṉiyik kuṟikka muṟpaṭṭapōtu, ataṉi aṟinta vēḷ evvi, aṉṉikku ettuṇiyō pala nayamoḻikaḷik kūṟi aṭakkavum, avaṉ titiyaṉoṭu pōr toṭuttu muṭivil tōṟṟaḻintāṉ; mēlik kaṭaṟkariyiṉiṉṟum pōntu mīkoṅku nāṭṭil taṅki vāymoḻi piḻiyātavar eṉṟa pukaḻkoṇṭu kōcar eṉpavar viḷakka muṟṟiruntaṉar; avarkaḷ orukālattil pāpilōṉiyā nāṭṭu vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta kiciyareṉṟa iṉattavar; avarkaḷ mēlikkaṭal vaḻiyāka vantu tamiḻakattu mēlik kaṭaṟkariyil taṅki villēruḻavarāy vāḻntaṉar. piṉṉar avarkaḷ pālikkāṭṭup piḷavu vaḻiyāka uḷnāṭṭil pukuntu, cērarkkum pāṇṭiyarkkum tāṉi maṟavarāyp paṇiceytu oḻukiṉar. orukāl, kōcaratu kuṟumpu mikka pōtu, cōḻaṉ polampūṇ kiḷḷi yeṉpavaṉ avarkaḷ cerukkiṉi muḷiyilē kiḷḷi, eṉṟumē avarkaḷ  tamiḻ
vēntarkkup paṇiyāḷarāy iruntoḻiyumāṟu ceytāṉ. akkiḷḷiyiṉ kālattil kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam iruntu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. oru kālattē tamiḻvēntari veṉṟu, avar nilattuḷ oru pakutiyik kippaṭuttik koṇṭa taḻumpaṉ eṉpavaṉatu talinakar ūṇūr eṉṟum avvūṇūrkkaku appāl uḷḷatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu maruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam eṉṟum maturi nakkīraṉār kūṟukiṉṟār. ayiriyāṟu vaṭukavēntaṉāṉa erumiyiṉ nāṭṭil (micūr nāṭṭil) uḷatu eṉpatum toṇṭittuṟi cēramāṉ kuṭṭuvaṟkuriyatu eṉpatum, toṇṭimāṉ polampūṇ tiriyaṉuṭiya pavattiri eṉṉum nakaram toṇṭi nāṭṭil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉamiyum ivarāl kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṟṟu
nāṭṭaracar cilar tamiḻaroṭu porutaṟku eḻuva taṟinta cōḻaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ etirceṉṟu porutu avari valiyaḻittu mīṇṭāṉāka. avveṟṟi kuṟittup pāṇṭiyaṉ māṟaṉum cēramāṉ kōtiyum kūṭalnakarkkaṇ veṟṟikoṇṭāṭi
yatum, taṉ nāṭṭuṭ pukuntu kuṟumpu ceytoḻukiya koṅkari veṉṟami kuṟittup pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṭalnakark kaṇ viḻāvayarntatum nakkīrar kāṭṭum ceytik kuṟippukkaḷākum. ivarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa maruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam, pāṇṭināṭṭu muttūṟṟuk kūṟṟattuk kaṭaṟkari ūrkaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉa irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattut tiṭṭāntatāṉapurattuk kal
veṭṭukkaḷāl1 terikiṟatu; āyiṉum, itaṟku vaṭapālil viḷaṅkiya ūṇūr kalveṭṭuk kālattēyē maṟintu pōṉatāka aṟikiṉṟōm; orukāl iṉi veḷivara irukkam kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappaṭutalum kūṭum. kaṭalaṭiyātu ilaṅki koṇṭa vaḷanāṭu eṉappaṭum kōṉāṭṭup pakuti
yiluḷḷa maruṅkūr1 paṭṭiṉamākāmiyiṉ, ataṉi īṇṭuk kōṭaṟku iṭamilli. erumināṭṭu ayiriyāṟu ippōtu micūr nāṭṭu ācaṉ māvaṭṭattil uḷatu; kuṭṭuvaṉatu toṇṭi, maliyāḷa māvaṭṭattuk kuṟumparnāṭu vaṭṭattil kaṭaṟkariyil irukkiṟatu. pavattiri nakaram nellūr māvaṭṭattuk kūṭūr vaṭṭattuk kaṭaṟkari nakarāyiruntu kaṭal kōṭku iriyāki viṭṭatu; ataṉi am māvaṭṭattu reṭṭipāḷiyam kalveṭṭukkaḷ "cayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa maṇṭalattuk kaṭalkoṇṭa pavattirik kōṭṭam"2 eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. karikāṟ cōḻaṉatu āṭci mēṟkil maliyamāṉ nāṭuvariyil parantiruntamiyiṉ, annāṭṭiṉ kīḻp pakutiyāṉa tirumuṉippāṭināṭṭu iṭiyāṟu eṉṉum ūr akkālattē ciṟanta nakaramāka viḷaṅkiya ceytiyi nakkīraṉār kuṟikkiṉṟār. pāṇṭināṭṭu viyik kariyil viḷaṅkiya tirumarutan tuṟi pōlat teṉpeṉṉi kariyil ivviṭiyāṟu tiru marutan
tuṟiyāka viḷaṅkiṟ ṟeṉpati avvūrk kal veṭṭukkaḷ tirumuṉippāṭi nāṭṭu iṭiyāṟṟūr nāṭṭu iṭi
yāṟuṭiya tirumarutantuṟi3 eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. maturi māvaṭṭattup periyakuḷam pakutiyiluḷḷa kuḷḷapurattuk kalveṭṭukkaḷ viyit tuṟiyit "tirumarutantuṟi"4 eṉṟu iyampukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam pāṇṭināṭṭut tirumarutan tuṟi pōlat tirumuṉippāṭi nāṭṭut tirumarutantuṟi pulavar pāṭum pukaḻpeṟṟami paṟṟi, "perumpeyark karikāl vel
pōrc cōḻaṉ iṭiyāṟṟaṉṉa nallici veṟukki"5 eṉṟu ataṉ celvamikutiyic ciṟappittup pāṭiyuḷḷār. annāḷil tamiḻar koṇṭāṭiya kārttiki viḻāvi, "kuṟumuyal maṟuniṟam kiḷara mati niṟintu aṟumīṉ cērum akaliruḷ naṭunāḷ, maṟuku viḷakkuṟuttu māli tūkkip paḻaviṟal mūtūrp palaruṭaṉ tuvaṉṟiya viḻā" eṉṟu pārāṭṭik kūṟuvar. ivar kālattē kuṭapulattu viripporuḷum, vaṭapulattuc cantaṉakkallum, vēṅkaṭamaliyiṉ vēṅki maramum tamiḻakattil taliciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. ivar pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ ēṉit toki nūl
kaḷilum uḷḷaṉa.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukivarum talimakaṉ, varivu nikaḻtaṟku muṉ talimakaḷip pirintu cella vēṇṭiya kaṭami
yuṭiyaṉāki ataṉi avaṭkum terivittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ. āyiṉum, avaṉatu ciṉṉāḷip pirivu, avaṭkup peru varuttattic ceytatu. avaṉ tāṉ kuṟitta vaṇṇamē cila nāṭkaḷil taṉ kaṭamiyi muṭittukkoṇṭu mīṇṭu pōntāṉ. avaṉ talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattē vantu niṉṟāṉāka, tōḻi aṟintu koṇṭu talivikku aṟiviyāmal orupuṟattē avaḷi niṟuttit tāṉ kūṟuvatu avaṉ cevip
paṭumāṟu uriyāṭaluṟṟu "itō pār, kaṭaliṉkaṇ iṟā mīṉit tēṭik koṇarnta nīrkkākki taṉ peṭiyi aḻittu ataṉ vāyil am mīṉit tarukiṟatu; ivvāṟu puḷḷiṉam tammiṟ pirintum pirivāṟṟiyum piṉṉark kūṭiyum iṉpuṟutaṟku iṭaṉākiya ittuṟi ettuṇi iṉpamāka uḷatu; itaṉik kaṇṭu  nam talivarum viriviṟ poruḷ tēṭik koṇarntu tirumaṇattāl pēraruḷ ceyvar eṉakkoṇṭu āṟṟiyiruppatē namakku nalamāvatu" eṉṟāḷ. atu kēṭṭa talivi, "nī kūṟumāṟu ittuṟi eṉakku iṉpam ceytatu, kātalaṉoṭu kaḷavut toṭarpu uṇṭātaṟku muṉpu, kāṇ; ippōtu ittuṟiyum piṟavum tarum kāṭci āṟṟāmiyē payakkiṉṟatu; eṉ ceyvēṉ" eṉṟāḷ.
iv vuriyāṭṭāl, kātaṟ kūṭṭat toṭarpiṉkaṇ iṉimi nalkum kāṭci palavum, kātalar piriviṉkaṇ at toṭarpiyē niṉippittu varuttum eṉpatu pulappaṭak kaṇṭa āciriyar nakkīraṉār ataṉi ippāṭṭiṉkaṇ amittup pāṭukiṉṟār.
māyirum puṇari1 tuṇiya nōkkic
cēyiṟā eṟinta ciṟuveṇ kākki
pāyirum paṉikkaḻi tuḻiip piṅkāl
tāṉvīḻ pēṭikkup 2payiriṭūuc cēkkum 
ciṟuvī ñāḻal tuṟiyumār iṉitē 
perumpulam puṟṟa neñcamoṭu 3palaniṉintu
yāṉum iṉiyēṉ 4āṉā tumaṇar
vēṟupaṉ ṉāṭṭil kāltara vanta
palavuṟu paṇṇiyam iḻitarum nilavumaṇal
neṭuñciṉip puṉṉik kaṭuñcūl veṇkuruku
uravuttiri yōtam verūum
perutīrc cērppaṉoṭu maṇavā ūṅkē.
itu, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat talivi vaṉpuṟi etiraḻintatu.
uri
māyirum puṇari tuṇiya nōkki - periya kariya kaṭaliṭattu alikaḷ kontaḷippiṉṟit teḷintu niṟkum cevvi kaṇṭu; cēy iṟā eṟinta ciṟuveṇkākki - cevviya iṟāmīṉkaḷ mēya avaṟṟi vēṭṭiyāṭiya ciṟiya veṇmiyāṉa niṟattiyuṭiya nīrkkākki; pāyirum paṉikkaḻi tuḻii - paranta kariya kuḷirnta kaḻiyiṉ nīrit tuḻāvi; tāṉ vīḻpiṅkāṟ pēṭikku - taṉṉāl kātalikkappaṭṭa peṭik kākkiyi; payir iṭūuc cēkkum - payirkural iṭṭu aḻittu ataṉ pakkal taṅkit tāṉ kavarntu pōnta iriyi nalkum; ciṟuvī ñāḻal tuṟiyumār iṉitu - ciṟu pūkkaḷiyuṭiya ñāḻal maraṅkaḷ niṟkum nīrttuṟiyum iṉitākavē iruntatu; perumpulam puṟṟa neñcamoṭu āṉātu - periya toru taṉimit tuyaruṟṟa neñciṉōṭu amintoḻiyāmal; pala niṉintu - palappala eṇṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭu; yāṉum iṉiyēṉ - yāṉum varunti aḻutal illēṉākavē yiruntēṉ;  umaṇar vēṟu paṉṉāṭṭil kāltara vanta - uppu vaṇikarāl vēṟu palavākiya nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vaṇṭikaḷil ēṟṟik koṇarap
paṭṭu vanta; palavuṟu paṇṇiyam iḻitarum - palavāy mikka paṇṭaṅkaḷ iṟaṅkum; nilavu maṇal neṭuñciṉip puṉṉi- veṇmaṇal paranta tuṟiyiṭattu niṟkum neṭiya kiḷi
kaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉi marattil taṅkum; kaṭuñcūl veṇkuruku - taliccūl koṇṭa veṇkuruku; uravut tiri ōtam verūum - parantuvarum alikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭaṟ perukkukku añci nīṅkum; perunīrc cērppaṉoṭu maṇavā vūṅku - periya kaṭaṉilac cērppaṉākiya talivaṉoṭu kūṭi makiḻtaṟku muṉpellām, e-ṟu.
cērppaṉoṭu maṇāvā vūṅku yāṉum iṉiyēṉ; tuṟiyumār iṉitu; maṇanta ippōtu yāṉum āṉātu iṉivēṉāyiṉēṉ; tuṟiyum varuttam payappa tāyiṟṟu eṉa iciyeccattāl vēṇṭuvaṉa peytu kūṭṭi viṉi muṭivu ceyka. kāṟṟiṉ iyakkam kuṉṟiyapōtu alikaḷ iṉṟit teḷintu niṟkum nīrpparappil iṟāmīṉ mēyum eṉpa. akkālamaṟintu nīrkkākki ceṉṟu avaṟṟi vēṭṭiyāṭi uṇṇumātaliṉ, māyirum puṇari tuṇiya nōkkic cēy iṟā eṟinta ciṟuveṇkākki 
eṉṟār. alikaḷāl kalakkuṟāta pōtu nīr teḷintu tōṉṟutalāl puṇari tuṇiya eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. puṇari, ali. peruṅkāṟṟāl alikaḷ eḻuntu alikkum kālattu mīṉkaḷ palavum kaṭaliṉ aṭiyil ceṉṟoḷikkum; ciṟu kurukukaḷ akkāṟṟukku añci avviṭam cellā; ātalāl nīrkkākki aliyiṉṟi amiti cāṉṟa kaṭaṟ
parappi nōkkum eṉṟu aṟika. tuṇital, teḷital. mīṉ vēṭṭam ceṉṟu mīṇṭa nīrkkākki kaḻiyiṭattu vāḻum mīṉkaḷit taṉ kātaṟpeṭiyiṉ poruṭṭuk kavarntu koṇṭu varukiṟa teṉpār tāṉ vīḻpiṅkāṟ pēṭikkup pāyirum paṉikkaḻit tuḻii eṉṟār. kavarnta mīṉit tāṉ uṇṇātu taṉ peṭikkeṉak koṇartaliṉ, peṭiyi aḻittut taruvatāyiṟṟu eṉṟaṟkup payiriṭūuc cēkkum eṉṟār. payirtal, aḻittal, cēkkutal, taṅkutal, vēṟu vēṟu vēntar āḷum palavā yuḷḷa nāṭukaḷ eṉpār, vēṟu paṉṉāṭu eṉṟār. paṇṇiyam, paṇṭaṅkaḷ, kāl vaṇṭi, puṉṉiyiṉ neṭuñciṉikkaṇ iruntatāyiṉum kaṭuñcūl uṭimiyiṉ, añcuvatāyiṟ ṟeṉpār. kaṭuñcūl veṇkuruku uravuttiri yōtam verūum eṉṟār. kaṭuñcūl taliccūl; putumip poruṭṭāya kaṭiyeṉṉum col kaṭuñcūl eṉat tirintu muṭintatu. perunīr, kaṭal maṇam īṇṭuk kātaluṟaviṉ mēṟṟu.
variviṭi vittup pirintu mīṇṭa talimakaṉ, talivi maṉiyiṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉāṉatu aṟinta tōḻi, taliviyatu kātalmikutiyiyum āṟṟāmiyiyum avaṟku nērē kāṭṭi varivu kaṭāvaluṟṟut taliviyit tām nikaḻttum uri
kaḷ avaṉ cevippaṭumaḷavil ōriṭattē niṟuttic ciṟu
veṇkākki kaṭaliṭattē iṟāmīṉ vēṭṭam purintu pōntu kaḻiyiṉkaṇ tuḻāvit taṉ peṭikkēṟṟa iriyit tēṭik koṇarntu appeṭiyit taṉpāl varuvittu nalkum ikkaṭaṟṟuṟi ettuṇi iṉimi tarukiṟatu kāṇ eṉṟum, akkākkiyip pōlavē talimakaṉum akaliṭattē poruḷvayiṟ pirintu ceṉṟu varivukkuriya poruḷit tantu niṉṉi varintu koḷḷac camintuḷḷāṉ eṉpati uḷḷuṟuttum kūṟiṉāḷ. atu kēṭṭa talimakaḷ uḷḷuṟiyik koḷḷāmal kākki taṉ peṭiyiṉpāl aṉpu celuttum iṉiyakāṭci aminta ittuṟi, talimakaṉik kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koḷḷutaṟku muṉpellām eṉakku iṉpam tantu niṉṟatu eṉpāḷ, māyirum puṇari tuṇiya nōkkic cēyiṟā eṟinta ciṟu veṇkākki pāyirum paṉikkaḻi tuḻiip piṅkāl tāṉ vīḻ pēṭikkup payiriṭūuc cēkkum ciṟuvī ñāḻal tuṟiyumār iṉitē.... perunīrc cērppaṉoṭu maṇavā vūṅkē eṉat tōḻi kūṟṟik koṇṭeṭuttu moḻintāḷ. perunīrc cērppaṉoṭu maṇavāvūṅkut tuṟi iṉitāyiruntatu. eṉavē maṇantoḻukum ippōtu attuṟi taṉakku iṉṉātāyiṟṟeṉat talivi kūṟiṉāḷā
yiṟṟu. talivi kūṟṟāmiṭattuk kaṭaliṉ amiti kaṇṭu ciṟuveṇkākki iṟāmīṉ vēṭṭam purintatupōla āyamum tōḻiyum nīṅkit tāṉ niṉṟa taṉimi kaṇṭu pōntu, talimakaṉ taṉṉit tali maṇantu kātaluṟavu nalki nalaṉ nukarntāṉ; iṉṟu avaṉ pirivāl yāṉ kiyaṟṟu varuntukiṉṟēṉ eṉa uḷḷuṟi koḷḷappaṭum. avaṉatu pirivi āṟṟum vaṉmi eṉpāl iṉmiyiṉ, taṉimittuyar eṉṉip peritum varuttukiṉṟamiyiṉ yāṉ iṭiyaṟāta,  aḻukiyi mēviṉēṉ eṉpāḷ, perumpulam puṟṟa neñcamoṭu palapulantu yāṉum āṉātu iṉiku
vēṉāyiṉēṉ eṉṟāḷ. maṇavāvūṅku iṉiyēṉ eṉavē, ippōtu āṉātu iṉikuvēṉāyiṉēṉ eṉpatām. iṉital, aḻutal, uvānāḷiṟ peruki varum kaṭalōtattālum citivuṟāta maṇal mēṭu eṉṟaṟku umaṇar vēṟu paṉṉāṭṭil kāltara vanta palavuṟu paṇṇiyam iḻitarum nilavumaṇal eṉṟum, aṅku niṉṟa puṉṉiyiṉ neṭuṅkōṭṭil iruppatāl veṇkuruku ōtattukku añcutal vēṇṭā eṉiṉum, veruvutaṟkētu atu taliccūl koṇṭiruttalē eṉṟaṟkuk kaṭuñcūl  veṇkuruku eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kātalar pirivāṟṟi yāṉ maṉikkaṇ ṇiruppiṉum kātaṟperumiyāl eṉ mēṉi eytum vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu ayaṟpeṇṭir tūṟṟum alarkku añcu
kiṉṟēṉ eṉṟa karuttu, neṭuñciṉip puṉṉik kaṭuñcūl veṇkuruku uravuttiri yōtam verūum eṉṟataṉkaṇ uḷḷuṟuttappaṭṭu uḷatu. iḵtu alar aṟivuṟuttut talivaṉi varivukaṭāyatu.
32. kapilar
kaḷavuneṟiyiṟ kātaṟṟoṭarpu koṇṭa talimakkaḷ, tōḻiyiṉ naṭpip peṟṟālaṉṟi oruvariyoruvar taṉittuk kaṇṭu iṉpuṟutaṟku vaḻiyilli eṉṟu teḷintaṉar. atupaṟṟit talivi kāṭṭiya kuṟippāl talimakaṉ tōḻiyi aṟintu
koṇṭu avaḷoṭu collāṭit taṉ kātaṟ karuttit terivittāṉ. avvāṟu talimakaḷ taṉ kātal uṟavit taṉakkup pulappaṭuttāmiyāl, avaṉip palavakiyāl ārāyntu talimakaṟkum talimakaṭkum iṭiyē muṉṉuṟavu uṇṭeṉa ōrntu koṇṭāḷ; āyiṉum, ataṉit talivitāṉ aṟiyap pulappaṭuttuvataṉ muṉ oṉṟum ceytaṟku iṉmiyiṉ avaḷ ataṉi veḷiyiṭaṟkēṟṟa vakiyil tōḻi uriyāṭaluṟṟāḷ. talimakaḷō nāṇamikutiyāl oṉṟum aṟiyāḷ pōlavē oḻukiṉāḷ. orunāḷ talivaṉatu varavu kaṇṭa tōḻi, talimakaḷit taṉpāl aḻittu, "atō tōṉṟum veḷḷiya aruviyik kāṇ; ammalik kuriyaṉākiya talivaṉ nammi nayantu ivviṭattukku vantu varuttattōṭu celkiṟāṉ; yāṉ ataṉip paṉmuṟiyum niṉakkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ; nīyō eṉ collit teḷi iṉṟāyilli; iṉi, oṉṟu ceyka; nīyē ceṉṟu avaṉ varuttattik kaṇṭu niṉakku uyirttōḻarāvārōṭu ārāyntu vēṇṭuvaṉa aṟintu naṭpuc ceytukoḷvatu naṉṟu; uṇṭākiya piṉpu naṭpup piritaṟku amivataṉṟu; atupaṟṟiyē naṭpuc ceyyum periyōr, muṉkūṭṭiyē naṉku ārāyntu teḷiveytiya piṉpu oruvarpāl naṭpuk koḷvarē yaṉṟi, naṭpuc ceytu koṇṭa piṉpu ārāyvatu ceyyār; ceyyiṉ atu perumi payavātu eṉpatu karuttu" eṉṟu iyampiṉāḷ. atu kēṭṭa talimakaḷ uvakiyāl muṟuvalittu, "eṉakku niṉṉiṉum ciṟantār piṟar iṉmiyiṉ itaṉi nīyē ceyka" eṉṟāḷ.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, peṇmip paṇpāl nāttaṭippaṭṭirunta talivikkuc cāṉṟōr cāṉṟāṇmi kūṟumāṟṟāmal vāytiṟantu pēcumāṟu ceyta tōḻiyiṉ matinuṭpam viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭa kapilar ataṉi ippāṭṭiṭit toṭuttup pāṭiyuḷḷār.
māyōṉ aṉṉa mālvarik kavāaṉ
vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi
ammali kiḻavōṉ1 nannayantu eṉṟum
varuntiṉaṉ 2eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy
nīyuṅ kaṇṭu 3 numaroṭum eṇṇi
aṟivaṟin taḷaval vēṇṭum maṟutaraṟku
ariya vāḻi tōḻi periyōr
nāṭi naṭpi ṉallatu
naṭṭu nāṭār tam 4 oṭṭiyōr tiṟattē
itu, tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu.
uri
māyōṉ aṉṉa mālvarik kāvaaṉ - māyōṉākiya kaṇṇaṉ pōlum tōṟṟattiyuṭiya periya malippakkattil; vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅku veḷḷaruvi - palarāmaṉ pōla viḷaṅkum veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiyuṭiya; ammali kiḻavōṉ - aḻakiya mali nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉ; nam nayantu eṉṟum varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy - nammi virumpi nāḷum peruntuyar uḻantaṉaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatākiya vāyuriyi nī teḷikiṉṟāyilli; nīyum kaṇṭu - ataṉi nīyum nēril kaṇṭu; numaroṭum eṇṇi - niṉakkuc ciṟanta tamarāyiṉārōṭu ārāyntu; aṟivaṟintu aḷaval vēṇṭum - aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi naṉku aṟintu aḷavaḷāvik koḷḷal vēṇṭum; tōḻi; vāḻi; maṟutaraṟku ariya - naṭpāṟ kalantapiṉ pirintu nīṅkutal aritākalāṉ; periyōr - periyarāyiṉār; nāṭi naṭpiṉallatu - oruvari mutaṟkaṇ ārāyntu naṭpuc ceytavarē yaṉṟi; naṭṭu tam oṭṭiyōr tiṟattu nāṭār - naṭpuc ceyta piṉpu tammoṭu poruntiṉōriṭattu nalantīṅkukaḷi ārāyvatu ilar. e-ṟu.
kiḻavōṉ varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy ākaliṉ, tōḻi, nīyum kaṇṭu, eṇṇi aṟintu, aḷaval vēṇṭum; maṟutaraṟku ariya, periyōr nāṭi naṭpiṉallatu naṭṭut tam oṭṭiyōr tiṟattu nāṭār eṉak kūṭṭi viṉimuṭivu ceyka. cēymiyil tōṉṟum mali pacuṅkāṭu pōrttu nīlaniṟaṅkoṇṭu tōṉṟuvatu paṟṟi ataṉi māyōṉ aṉṉa mālvariyeṉṟum, ataṉkaṇ veḷḷi niṟattuṭaṉ iḻiyum aruvikku akkaṇṇaṉoṭu uṭaṉ vittuk kūṟappaṭum palarāmaṉi uvamittu vāli
yōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅku veḷḷaruvi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉpatu, īṇṭuc ceyappaṭu poruṇmittāy eṉappaṭuvatu eṉa niṉṟatu. vāyccol, meymmiyuri. tēṟutal, teḷital, numar, niṉakkuc ciṟantavar. aṟivaṟintu aḷava
lāvatu, "kuṇanāṭik kuṟṟamum nāṭi avaṟṟuḷ miki nāṭi mikka koḷal"1 maṟutaral, mīḷutal; īṇṭup pirintu nīṅkutal mēṟṟu periyōr, naṟkuṇa naṟceykikaḷāl perumi
yuṭiya rāyiṉōr. "nāṭātu naṭṭaliṉ kēṭilli naṭṭapiṉ vīṭilli naṭpāḷpavarkku"2 eṉpatu iṅku oppu nōkkaṟkuriyatu. vīṭilli eṉpatu maṟutaraṟku ariya eṉa viḷakkap
paṭṭatu. vāḻi, muṉṉili aci. maṟutaral, mīḷutal, pirital.
talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talimakaḷi uṭaṉpaṭuttaṟku muyalukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, taliviyoṭu collāṭaluṟṟu, avaṉatu maliyiyum, aruviyiyum kāṭṭi, ammali kiḻavōṉ eṉpavaḷ, kavāṉum aruviyum koṇṭu perumikku uriya kaṇṇaṉiyum palarāmaṉiyum niṉippikkum tiṟam cuṭṭi, māyōṉ aṉṉa mālvarik kavāaṉ, vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅku veḷḷaruvi ammali kiḻavōṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ nammi nayantu ivaṇ pōntu, iṭam vāyāmiyāl varuntic ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu poyyaṉṟu. meyyuri eṉpāḷ, nam nayantu eṉṟum varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatu ōr vāyccol eṉṟum, ataṉi yāṉ urittavaḻi uḷḷaṅkoḷḷāyā
yiṉi eṉpāḷ tēṟāy eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. eṉ vāyc
collit tēṟāyākaliṉ iṉi nīyē nēriṟ kaṇṭum niṉakkuc ciṟanta tamarāyiṉāri ārāyntum uṇmi teḷintu koḷka eṉpāḷ, nīyum kaṇṭu numaroṭum eṇṇi aṟivaṟintu aḷaval vēṇṭum eṉṟāḷ. aḷaval vēṇṭum eṉpatu, itu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷaviyāyt tōḻi talivi eṉṟa irupālum porunta niṉṟatu1. atu kēṭṭatum talivikku uḷḷattē acivu piṟantatu. avaṭkum talimakaṉukkum uṇṭāṉa uṟaviṉit tāṉ aṟintuḷḷamiyit talimakaṭkut terivippataṉṟi vēṟu ceyaliṉmiyiṉ, ivvārāycciyi iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉpē ceytaṟkuriya nī ataṟkup piṉṉarc ceyvatu perumi yākātu eṉṟu teruṭṭuvāḷāy, periyōr nāṭi naṭpiṉallatu naṭṭu nāṭār tam oṭṭiyōr tiṟattē eṉṟāḷ. varuntiṉaṉ eṉṟa eṉ vāyccol eṉṉātu varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatu ōr vāyccol eṉṟatu, eṉṉi yoḻittu numaroṭu eṇṇiṉum iccol vāyccol eṉṟē teḷiyappaṭum eṉṟaṟku talivaṉōṭu uṇṭākiya kātaluṟavit taṉakkut talivi aṟiviyāmiyi uyttuṇarum poruṭṭut taṉṉi ayaṉmippaṭutti neruṅki moḻitalāl nī eṉavum numar eṉavum pirittōtiṉāḷ. iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ nikaḻnta kūṭṭamum naṭpum piriyat takuvaṉavalla eṉpāḷ maṟutaraṟku ariya eṉṟum, appirivu nikaḻtalit tāṉ virumpāmi tōṉṟa vāḻi eṉṟum tōḻi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. oṭṭiyōr eṉṟatu muṉṉuṟavu uṇmi tāṉ aṟintami tōḻi pulappaṭuttum kuṟippu.
"nāṟṟamum tōṟṟumum"1 eṉat toṭaṅkum nūṟpāvil varum, "maṟintavaḷ arukat, taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum mutal mūṉṟaḷi ippiṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅ
kiṉum" eṉṉum pakutikku itaṉi eṭuttōtik kāṭṭuvar iḷampūraṇar; nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum2 ippakutikkēkāṭṭuvar.


33. iḷavēṭṭaṉār
iḷavēṭṭaṉār eṉṟa peyaruṭiya cāṉṟōr iruvar toki nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar; avaruḷ iccāṉṟōr oruvar; ēṉiyōr maturi aṟuvivāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār eṉappaṭuvar; iṅṅaṉam ciṟappikkappaṭuvatu nōkkātu iruvariyum oruvarākak koḷvōrum uṇṭu. nalvēṭṭaṉār eṉṟum nākaṉ vēṭṭaṉār eṉṟum cāṉṟōr palar kāṇap
paṭutaliṉ vēṭṭaṉ eṉpatu paṇṭināḷil makkaḷiṭiyē payila vaḻaṅkiṉami naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. iṭikkālak kalveṭṭukkaḷilum ippeyaruṭiyār palar kāṇappaṭu
